//! Release activation extends the existing engine transaction and generation.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#5-hot-activation-and-ownership — CAS includes every activation, not only semantic changes.
use super::*;
use rusqlite::OptionalExtension;

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct ReleaseActivation {
    pub release: u64,
    pub expected_previous: Option<u64>,
    pub expected_revision: Option<u64>,
    pub restore_dormant: bool,
    pub native_digest: Option<String>,
    pub operation: String,
}
impl ReleaseActivation {
    pub fn new(release: u64, expected_previous: Option<u64>) -> Self {
        Self {
            release,
            expected_previous,
            expected_revision: None,
            restore_dormant: true,
            native_digest: None,
            operation: "activate".into(),
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn check(&self, conn: &rusqlite::Connection) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let actual: Option<String> = conn
            .query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key='release'", [], |r| {
                r.get(0)
            })
            .optional()?;
        if actual
            .as_ref()
            .is_some_and(|s| s.parse::<u64>().ok() != self.expected_previous)
        {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Generation("current release changed; run `snapback4 releases <dir>` and retry the selected release".into()));
        }
        let revision: Option<String> = conn
            .query_row(
                "SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key='release_revision'",
                [],
                |r| r.get(0),
            )
            .optional()?;
        let revision = revision.and_then(|v| v.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0);
        if self.expected_revision.is_some_and(|n| n != revision) {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Generation("activation revision changed (including rollback/equal-content activation); run `snapback4 releases <dir>` and retry".into()));
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

impl Engine {
    pub fn new_release(
        store: Store,
        schema: Schema,
        programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>,
        release: u64,
        expected_previous: Option<u64>,
    ) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        Self::new_release_with_feed_adoption(
            store,
            schema,
            programs,
            release,
            expected_previous,
            false,
        )
    }
    pub fn new_release_with_feed_adoption(
        store: Store,
        schema: Schema,
        programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>,
        release: u64,
        expected_previous: Option<u64>,
        allow_feed_adoption: bool,
    ) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        Self::new_release_prepared(
            store,
            schema,
            programs,
            &ReleaseActivation::new(release, expected_previous),
            allow_feed_adoption,
        )
    }
    pub fn new_release_prepared(
        store: Store, schema: Schema, programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>,
        options: &ReleaseActivation, allow_feed_adoption: bool,
    ) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        Self::new_release_prepared_at(store,schema,programs,options,allow_feed_adoption,crate::now_ms())
    }
    pub fn new_release_prepared_at(
        mut store: Store,
        mut schema: Schema,
        programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>,
        options: &ReleaseActivation,
        allow_feed_adoption: bool,
        now: i64,
    ) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        prepare(&mut schema)?;
        let programs = programs.into_iter().map(|p| (p.name.clone(), p)).collect();
        let (generation, metadata) = Self::activate(
            &mut store,
            &schema,
            &programs,
            Some(options),
            now,
            allow_feed_adoption,
            None,
            None,
        )?;
        store.exclude_ephemeral(&schema);
        Ok(Self {
            timeline_schedule: timeline::Schedule::default(),
            ephemeral: std::sync::Arc::new(ephemeral::Hub::new(
                metadata.store_id,
                generation,
                &schema,
            )),
            store,
            schema,
            programs,
            generation,
            expiry_after: BTreeMap::new(),
            dev_effects: false,
            ceilings: Ceilings::default(),
            entropy: crate::ids::seed(),
            minter: crate::ids::Mint::default(),
            #[cfg(feature = "native")]
            native: None,
            #[cfg(feature = "native")]
            native_logs: native::Logs::default(),
            presence: None,
        })
    }
    /// No replacement engine, post-commit SQL read, or fallible ephemeral swap.
    pub fn adopt_release(
        &mut self, schema: Schema, programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>,
        options: &ReleaseActivation, allow_feed_adoption: bool,
    ) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        self.adopt_release_at(schema,programs,options,allow_feed_adoption,crate::now_ms())
    }
    pub fn adopt_release_at(
        &mut self,
        mut schema: Schema,
        programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>,
        options: &ReleaseActivation,
        allow_feed_adoption: bool,
        now: i64,
    ) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        prepare(&mut schema)?;
        let programs = programs.into_iter().map(|p| (p.name.clone(), p)).collect();
        drop(self.ephemeral.lock()?);
        let (generation, metadata) = Self::activate(
            &mut self.store,
            &schema,
            &programs,
            Some(options),
            now,
            allow_feed_adoption,
            None,
            None,
        )?;
        self.store.exclude_ephemeral(&schema);
        self.timeline_schedule = timeline::Schedule::default();
        self.schema = schema;
        self.programs = programs;
        self.generation = generation;
        self.expiry_after.clear();
        self.dev_effects = false;
        self.ephemeral
            .rebind_committed(metadata.store_id, generation, &self.schema);
        Ok(generation)
    }
    #[cfg(feature = "native")]
    pub fn set_prepared_native(&mut self, module: Option<std::sync::Arc<native::Module>>) {
        self.native = module;
    }
}

fn prepare(schema: &mut Schema) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    columns::validate_before_prepare(schema)?;
    schema.check_row_view_audiences()?;
    schema.prepare_contribution_indexes();
    schema.prepare_delivery_lookup();
    if let Some(issue) = crate::schema::validate(schema).first() {
        return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(format!(
            "{}: {}",
            issue.path, issue.message
        )));
    }
    Ok(())
}
//! Owner-mediated release publication in the running HTTP process.
use super::*;
use crate::release_control::Command;
#[cfg(unix)]
use crate::release_control::Listener;
use snapback4_core::engine::release::ReleaseActivation;

#[cfg(unix)]
pub(super) fn start(
    shared: Arc<Shared>,
    dir: std::path::PathBuf,
) -> Result<std::thread::JoinHandle<()>, String> {
    let store = shared
        .owner
        .as_ref()
        .ok_or("release control requires a persisted owner")?
        .path()
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let listener = Listener::bind(&dir, store)?;
    crate::lifecycle::managed_cleanup();
    crate::lifecycle::background("release-control", move || {
        listener.run(|command| apply(&shared, &dir, command))
    })
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#5-hot-activation-and-ownership — only assignments and committed rebind follow the database commit.
pub(super) fn apply(
    shared: &Shared,
    dir: &std::path::Path,
    command: Command,
) -> Result<Json, String> {
    match command {
        Command::Status {
            after,
            items_after,
            event,
        } => {
            let mut engine=lock(&shared.engine);
            let mut status=engine.store_mut().release_status(after,items_after.as_deref(),event).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            status["clock"]=json!({"now":shared.now(),"offsetMs":lock(&shared.clock).offset()});
            Ok(status)
        },
        Command::Prune {
            confirm,
            expected_revision,
        } => {
            let mut result = lock(&shared.engine)
                .store_mut()
                .prune_dormant(confirm, Some(expected_revision))
                .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            if let Some(items) = result["items"].as_array_mut() {
                items.truncate(16);
            }
            result["items_after"] = result["items"]
                .as_array()
                .and_then(|items| items.last())
                .map(|i| i["key"].clone())
                .unwrap_or(Json::Null);
            Ok(result)
        }
        Command::Activate {
            release,
            expected_release,
            expected_revision,
            digest,
            rollback,
            restore_dormant,
            allow_feed_adoption,
        } => {
            if release == 0 {
                return Err("release id must be positive".into());
            }
            let project = Project {
                root: dir.join("releases").join(release.to_string()),
            };
            if !std::fs::symlink_metadata(&project.root)
                .is_ok_and(|m| m.is_dir() && !m.file_type().is_symlink())
            {
                return Err(format!(
                    "release {release} is not an immutable release directory"
                ));
            }
            if crate::release_files::digest(&project.root)? != digest {
                return Err("staged release content changed; redeploy its exact source".into());
            }
            if project.sources().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.is_empty() {
                return Err(format!(
                    "release {release} has no Quarry source; select a complete immutable release"
                ));
            }
            let bound = project.compile().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
                return Err(bound
                    .diagnostics
                    .iter()
                    .map(ToString::to_string)
                    .collect::<Vec<_>>()
                    .join("\n"));
            }
            let prepared = crate::native::prepare(&project, &bound).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            crate::dev_worker::prepare_release(&project, &bound.schema)?;
            crate::release_files::link_store(&project.root)?;
            if crate::release_files::digest(&project.root)? != digest {
                return Err("release artifacts changed during preparation; retry after inspecting immutable source".into());
            }
            crate::release_files::durable(&project.root)?;
            let mut pointer =
                crate::release_files::Pointer::prepare(dir, release, expected_release, rollback)?;
            let options = ReleaseActivation {
                release,
                expected_previous: expected_release,
                expected_revision: Some(expected_revision),
                restore_dormant,
                native_digest: prepared.installed.as_ref().map(|m| m.digest.clone()),
                operation: if rollback { "rollback" } else { "deploy" }.into(),
            };
            let _requests=shared.release_requests.write();
            let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
            lock(&shared.readers).begin_adoption(&bound);
            let generation = match engine.adopt_release_at(
                bound.schema.clone(),
                bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()),
                &options,
                allow_feed_adoption,
                shared.now(),
            ) {
                Ok(generation) => generation,
                Err(error) => {
                    lock(&shared.readers).finish_adoption();
                    return Err(error.to_string());
                }
            };
            #[cfg(test)]
            test_work(&shared.release_committed);
            engine.set_prepared_native(prepared.module);
            engine.set_presence(Some(shared.presence.clone()));
            shared.refresh_time_audience_clock(&engine, shared.now());
            lock(&shared.readers).replace(&engine, &bound.refusal_sites);
            {
                let mut active = lock(&shared.bound);
                let mut advertised = lock(&shared.generation);
                let mut native = lock(&shared.native);
                shared.latency.adopt(&bound);
                *active = bound;
                *advertised = generation;
                *native = prepared.installed;
                let mut root = lock(&shared.release_root);
                *root = Some(project.root);
                shared.worker_revision.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Release);
            }
            crate::lifecycle::generation(generation);
            shared.publish_engine(&engine);
            // Completion I/O can fail, but never abandons the new runtime.
            pointer.finish(generation)?;
            snapback4_core::diagnostic!(
                "snapback4 serve: release {release} activated without restart as generation {generation}"
            );
            Ok(
                json!({"release":release,"generation":generation,"revision":expected_revision+1,"hot":true,"adoption":engine.store().adoption_report.compact(),"details":engine.store().adoption_report.summary(),"backfills":engine.store().adoption_report.backfills}),
            )
        }
    }
}

//! Operator preflight runs the real activation on a private disk-backed snapshot.
// REL3 owns dormant-visibility counts here; SURF clause narration lives in release_narration.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#92-exact-preview-and-operator-vocabulary — no live pointer, generation or audit changes.
use super::*;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
struct Snapshot(PathBuf);
impl Drop for Snapshot {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);
    }
}

pub(super) fn preview(
    store_path: &Path,
    root: PathBuf,
    release: u64,
    options: &Options,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    let bound = super::releases::bound_at(root.clone())?;
    let project = Project { root };
    let native = crate::native::prepare(&project, &bound).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    crate::dev_worker::prepare_release(&project,&bound.schema)?;
    let id = snapback4_core::ids::mint(snapback4_core::now_ms(), &mut snapback4_core::ids::Mint::default())
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let snapshot = Snapshot(std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("snapback4-release-preview-{id}")));
    snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&snapshot.0).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let path = snapshot.0.join("store.sqlite");
    // SQLite VACUUM INTO takes a coherent read snapshot even with a live WAL writer.
    // Busy/disk errors refuse this preview; never fall back to copying database bytes.
    if store_path.exists() {
    let source = snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(store_path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    source
        .conn()
        .execute("VACUUM INTO ?1", [path.to_string_lossy().as_ref()])
        .map_err(|e| {
            format!("snapshot preview failed (retry after store/disk becomes available): {e}")
        })?;
    drop(source);
    }
    let mut store = snapback4_core::Store::open(&path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let held = store
        .release_status(0, None, None)
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#4-staging-clock — preview uses the owning host's authoritative time, without minting or advancing anything.
    let activation_now=if crate::store_owner::inspector_address(store_path).is_some() {
        let status=crate::release_control::request(store_path.parent().ok_or("preview store has no deployment directory")?,crate::release_control::Command::Status{after:0,items_after:None,event:None})?;
        if status["revision"]!=held["revision"] {return Err("preview raced release activation; retry against the current store".into());}
        status["clock"]["now"].as_i64().ok_or("serving owner does not expose its activation clock; restart it with this CLI before preview")?
    }else{snapback4_core::now_ms()};
    let mut activation =
        snapback4_core::engine::release::ReleaseActivation::new(release, held["release"].as_u64());
    activation.expected_revision = held["revision"].as_u64();
    activation.native_digest = native.installed.map(|n| n.digest);
    activation.operation = "preview".into();
    activation.restore_dormant = !options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction") || options.flags.contains_key("restore-dormant");
    let mut steps = Vec::new();
    let estimate = store
        .adoption_estimate(&bound.schema)
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
        .map(|e| e.summary());
    let candidate_schema=bound.schema.clone();
    let candidate_programs:std::collections::BTreeMap<_,_>=bound.programs.iter().map(|p|(p.program.name.clone(),p.program.clone())).collect();
    let mut hazards=Vec::new();
    let mut notes=Vec::new();
    let prior:Option<(String,String)>=store.conn().query_row("SELECT schema,programs FROM _generations WHERE generation=(SELECT CAST(value AS INTEGER) FROM _meta WHERE key='generation')",[],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?))).ok();
    let prior_schema=prior.as_ref().map(|(schema,_)|serde_json::from_str::<snapback4_core::Schema>(schema).map_err(|e|e.to_string())).transpose()?;
    if let Some((schema,programs))=prior {
        let prior:snapback4_core::Schema=serde_json::from_str(&schema).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
        steps=snapback4_core::successor::classify(&prior,&candidate_schema);
        let programs:std::collections::BTreeMap<String,snapback4_core::ir::Program>=serde_json::from_str(&programs).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
        let target_label = format!("release {release}");
        let compatibility = super::release_narration::compatibility(&store, &prior, &candidate_schema, held["release"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0), &target_label, options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction"))?;
        for line in compatibility {
            if let Some(warning) = line.strip_prefix("WARNING ") { hazards.push(warning.to_owned()); }
            else { notes.push(line.trim_start_matches("note: ").to_owned()); }
        }
        let rollback=!options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction");
        let (behavior_warnings, behavior_notes) = super::release_narration::behavior(store_path, &store, &prior, &candidate_schema, &programs, &candidate_programs, &project, held["release"].as_u64(), &target_label, rollback)?;
        hazards.extend(behavior_warnings); notes.extend(behavior_notes);
        for name in prior.tables.keys().filter(|name|!candidate_schema.tables.contains_key(*name) && prior.maintain_for(name).is_none() && prior.tables[*name].ephemeral.is_none()) {
            let count:u64=store.conn().query_row("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM r WHERE t=(SELECT num FROM _catalog WHERE name=?1)",[name],|r|r.get::<_,i64>(0).map(|n|n as u64)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            if count>0 {hazards.push(format!("table {name}: {count} rows become dormant; {target_label} lacks this table's behavior; preserve it in a bridge or accept its absence"));}
        }
    }
    super::release_safeguards::capture(&store)?;
    let mut engine = snapback4_core::Engine::new_release_prepared_at(
        store,
        bound.schema,
        bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program),
        &activation,
        options.flags.contains_key("allow-feed-adoption"),
        activation_now,
    )
    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let mut planned = engine
        .store_mut()
        .release_status(0, None, None)
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    planned["items"] = serde_json::to_value(
        engine
            .store_mut()
            .dormant_inventory(None, usize::MAX)
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
    )
    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let items: Vec<snapback4_core::store::DormantItem> = serde_json::from_value(planned["items"].clone()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let mut effects = super::release_safeguards::warnings(engine.store(),prior_schema.as_ref(),&candidate_schema,&candidate_programs,&items,release,!options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction"))?;
    effects.append(&mut hazards); hazards=effects;
    for fill in &engine.store().adoption_report.backfills { notes.push(format!("backfill: {fill}; confirm these historical values before selecting release {release}")); }
    steps.retain(|step|!step.message.contains("engine capture"));
    for step in &mut steps {
        if step.change==snapback4_core::successor::Change::Narrowing {
            step.change=snapback4_core::successor::Change::Additive;
            step.message=format!("{}; active rows satisfy the candidate after dormant projection",step.message.split(';').next().unwrap_or(&step.message));
        }
        if let Some(position)=step.path.strip_prefix("maintains[").and_then(|s|s.strip_suffix(']')).and_then(|s|s.parse::<usize>().ok()) {
            let label=candidate_schema.maintains[position].label();
            let visits=engine.store().adoption_report.views.iter().find(|view|view.name==label).map(|v|v.rows).unwrap_or(0);
            step.message=format!("maintain {label}: rebuilt from {visits} source rows");
            step.change=snapback4_core::successor::Change::Additive;
        }
        let path=step.path.strip_prefix("tables.").unwrap_or(&step.path).replace(".columns.",".");
        let count=planned["items"].as_array().into_iter().flatten().find(|i|i["path"].as_str()==Some(path.as_str())).and_then(|i|i["rows"].as_u64()).unwrap_or(0);
        let unit = if step.path.contains(".columns.") { "cells" } else if step.path.contains(".indexes.") { "entries" } else { "rows" };
        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#11-wave-11-factual-release-narration — per-path prose uses the same disjoint counts as the inventory totals.
        let retained = if count == 0 && unit == "cells" {
            format!("no standalone {unit} become dormant (cells of any dormant rows are counted with those rows)")
        } else if count == 0 {
            format!("no {unit} become dormant")
        } else {
            format!("its {unit} become dormant ({count} {unit}; restored on forward)")
        };
        step.message=step.message.replace("its rows are lost", &retained).replace("its values are lost", &retained);
    }
    let checklist = super::release_bridge::checklist(held["release"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0),release,&hazards);
    if let Some(path) = options.flags.get("bridge") {
        let current = held["release"].as_u64().ok_or("a bridge needs a current deployed release")?;
        super::release_bridge::write(Path::new(path),store_path.parent().ok_or("no deployment directory")?,current,release,&checklist)?;
        eprintln!("bridge starter written to {path}; edit its current source and follow ROLLBACK-CHECKLIST.md before activation");
    }
    if options.json {
        println!("{}",serde_json::to_string_pretty(&serde_json::json!({"preview":true,"checklist":checklist,"hazards":hazards,"notes":notes,"current":held,"plan":planned,"steps":steps,"estimate":estimate,"phases":engine.store().adoption_report.summary(),"backfills":engine.store().adoption_report.backfills})).unwrap());
    } else {
        println!("preview: release {release} would be current as generation {}; live release unchanged", engine.generation());
        super::release_summary::print(prior_schema.as_ref(), &candidate_schema, &candidate_programs, &hazards, &notes, &steps, &items);
        if !options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction") { println!("  checklist: prove the named refusal cases and affected lists/counts as real accounts before release {release}; `--bridge <new-dir>` saves current source plus this app's ROLLBACK-CHECKLIST.md"); }
        println!("  storage: {}",snapback4_core::store::adoption_report::AdoptionReport::dormant(&items));
        if options.flags.contains_key("verbose") {
            for hazard in hazards { println!("  detail: WARNING {hazard}"); }
            for note in notes { println!("  detail: note: {note}"); }
            for step in steps {println!("  {}: {}",step.path,step.message);}
            for item in &items { println!("  {}: {} {} dormant (restored on compatible forward); sample ids [{}]", item.key, item.rows, item.unit(), item.sample_ids.join(", ")); }
            println!("{}", engine.store().adoption_report.summary());
            if let Some(estimate) = estimate {
                println!("{estimate}");
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#1 — the packaged proof kit owns hosts; builders own assertions.
import { mkdirSync, openSync, closeSync, writeSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { resolve } from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { createRequire } from "node:module";
import type { BrowserContext, BrowserType, LaunchOptions, Locator, Page } from "playwright-core";
import { resolveBinary } from "./bin-path.ts";
import { bounded, deadline, logged, logText, portRange, ready, stopChild, type ProofHost, type ProofOptions, logsDir } from "./proof-process.ts";
export { bounded, cleanup, stopChild, portRange } from "./proof-process.ts";
export type { ProofHost, ProofOptions } from "./proof-process.ts";

const fetchIdentity = async (url: string, timeoutMs: number) => {
  const response = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(timeoutMs) });
  return { response, body: await response.json() as Record<string, unknown> };
};
async function nativeHost(verb: "dev" | "serve", prefix: string[], options: ProofOptions): Promise<ProofHost & { personas?: string[] }> {
  const env = { ...process.env, ...options.env }, id = randomUUID();
  const ports = portRange(env), timeout = deadline(options.timeoutMs, 30_000);
  const args = [...(options.args ?? [])];
  if (!args.includes("--port")) args.push("--port", ports);
  const run = logged(resolveBinary(), [verb, ...prefix, ...args], { ...options, env: { ...env, SNAPBACK4_PROOF_ID: id, SNAPBACK4_SUPERVISOR_PID: String(process.pid) } });
  try {
    const url = await ready(run, line => {
      const match = line.match(/^SNAPBACK4_READY verb=(dev|serve) generation=\d+ url=(http:\/\/[^ ]+) pid=(\d+)(?: |$)/);
      if (!match) return;
      if (match[1] !== verb || Number(match[3]) !== run.child.pid) throw Error("proof ready identity mismatch");
      return match[2];
    }, timeout);
    const { response, body } = await fetchIdentity(`${url}/auth/config`, timeout);
    if (!response.ok || body.proof_id !== id) throw Error("proof served identity mismatch");
    let personas: string[] = [];
    if (verb === "dev" && body.devDoors !== false) {
      const status = await fetchIdentity(`${url}/dev/status`, timeout);
      if (status.body.pid !== run.child.pid) throw Error("proof dev PID mismatch");
      if (!Array.isArray(status.body.personas) || !status.body.personas.every(name => typeof name === "string")) throw Error("proof dev personas missing");
      personas = status.body.personas;
    }
    return { url, ...(verb === "dev" ? { personas } : {}), pid: run.child.pid!, logs: run.logs, stop: () => stopChild(run.child, deadline(options.stopTimeoutMs, 5000)) };
  } catch (error) { await stopChild(run.child, deadline(options.stopTimeoutMs, 5000)); throw error; }
}
export interface DevHost extends ProofHost {
  personas: string[];
  /** Read the current authoritative development time, including all clock advances. */
  now(): Promise<number>;
}
/** The array form preserves the former startDev(args) recipe. */
/** @throws invalid port range or conflicting memory flags; child exits, readiness deadline, PID/served identity mismatch, or unavailable dev clock. */
export function startDev(options: ProofOptions | readonly string[] = {}): Promise<DevHost> {
  const opts: ProofOptions = Array.isArray(options) ? { args: options } : options as ProofOptions;
  const args = [...(opts.args ?? [])];
  const persistent = args.some(arg => arg === "--store" || arg.startsWith("--store=") || arg === "--no-memory");
  if (persistent && args.includes("--memory")) throw new TypeError("proof startDev: --memory conflicts with --store/--no-memory");
  // Native persistence is the default. --no-memory remains a consumed explicit alias.
  const host = nativeHost("dev", [], { ...opts, args: args.filter(arg => arg !== "--no-memory") });
  return host.then(host => ({ ...host, personas: host.personas!, now: async () => {
    const { response, body } = await fetchIdentity(`${host.url}/dev/status`, deadline(opts.timeoutMs, 10_000));
    if (!response.ok || typeof body.now !== "number") throw Error("proof dev clock unavailable; password-only dev requires explicit --unsafe-dev-doors for clock/fixture tools; use serve staging for real users");
    return body.now;
  } }));
}
export interface ServeHost extends ProofHost {
  /** Authoritative staging time; requires a staging store and local operator authority. */
  now(): Promise<number>;
  /** Advance staging time once and drain due work. A lost reply must not be retried. */
  advance(duration: string, options?: { eachBoundary?: boolean }): Promise<number>;
  operatorToken(service?: string, days?: number): Promise<string>;
  seedAccounts(options: SeedAccountsOptions): Promise<StagingSessions>;
  sessions?: StagingSessions;
}
export interface ServeOptions extends ProofOptions { seedUsers?: SeedAccountsOptions; clockOffset?: string }
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#2-real-staging-accounts — operator authority stays in the existing local store proof; bulk setup refuses production.
/** @throws E_PROOF_USAGE for missing deployment directory; child exit, readiness deadline or served identity mismatch. */
export async function startServe(deployDir: string, options: ServeOptions | readonly string[] = {}): Promise<ServeHost> {
  if (typeof deployDir !== "string" || !deployDir.trim()) throw Object.assign(new TypeError("E_PROOF_USAGE: startServe(deployDir, options) requires a deployment directory as its first argument"), { code: "E_PROOF_USAGE" });
  const opts: ServeOptions = Array.isArray(options) ? { args: options } : options as ServeOptions;
  const directory = resolve(opts.cwd ?? ".", deployDir);
  if (opts.clockOffset !== undefined && (typeof opts.clockOffset !== "string" || !/^[+-]?\d+(ms|s|m|h|d|w)$/.test(opts.clockOffset))) throw new TypeError("E_PROOF_USAGE: clockOffset is a signed duration such as +25h");
  if (opts.clockOffset !== undefined && opts.args?.some(arg => arg === "--clock-offset" || arg.startsWith("--clock-offset="))) throw new TypeError("E_PROOF_USAGE: pass clockOffset once");
  const host = await nativeHost("serve", [directory], { ...opts, args: [...(opts.args ?? []), ...(opts.clockOffset === undefined ? [] : ["--clock-offset", opts.clockOffset])] });
  const clockCall = async (body?: object): Promise<number> => {
    const config = await fetchIdentity(`${host.url}/auth/config`, deadline(opts.timeoutMs, 10_000));
    if (!config.response.ok || config.body.environment !== "staging") throw Error("E_CLOCK_DEV_ONLY: clock controls require a deploy --staging store");
    const clockToken = served.operatorToken("clock");
    const response = await fetch(`${host.url}/dev/clock/${body ? "advance" : "status"}`, { method: body ? "POST" : "GET", headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${await clockToken}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" }, ...(body ? { body: JSON.stringify(body) } : {}), signal: AbortSignal.timeout(deadline(opts.timeoutMs, 60_000)) });
    const result = await response.json() as { now?: number; denied?: { code?: string; message?: string } };
    if (!response.ok || result.denied || !Number.isSafeInteger(result.now)) throw Error(`staging clock refused or reply unavailable; inspect clock and queued work before retrying: ${result.denied?.code ?? response.status} ${result.denied?.message ?? ""}`);
    return result.now!;
  };
  const served: ServeHost = { ...host,
    now: () => clockCall(),
    advance: (duration, options = {}) => {
      if (typeof duration !== "string" || !/^\+?\d+(ms|s|m|h|d|w)$/.test(duration)) throw new TypeError("E_PROOF_USAGE: advance requires a positive whole duration");
      return clockCall({ duration, eachBoundary: options.eachBoundary ?? false });
    },
    operatorToken: async (service = "operator", days = 1) => {
      if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$/.test(service) || !Number.isSafeInteger(days) || days < 1 || days > 3650) throw Error("proof operatorToken: invalid service or days (1..3650)");
      const result = await cli(["token", "--service", service, "--days", String(days), "--release", directory, "--quiet"], opts);
      if (result.code !== 0) throw Error(`proof operator token refused: ${result.stderr}`);
      const token = result.stdout.trim();
      if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$/.test(token)) throw Error("proof operator token malformed");
      return token;
    },
    seedAccounts: accountOptions => seedAccounts(served, accountOptions),
  };
  try { if (opts.seedUsers) served.sessions = await served.seedAccounts(opts.seedUsers); return served; }
  catch (error) { await served.stop(); throw error; }
}
async function frontend(kind: "static" | "vite", dir: string, options: ProofOptions): Promise<ProofHost> {
  const env = { ...process.env, ...options.env }, ports = portRange(env), timeout = deadline(options.timeoutMs, 30_000);
  const cwd = resolve(options.cwd ?? ".");
  const directory = resolve(cwd, dir);
  // Vite is resolved from the app, never the proof package's development dependencies.
  const vite = kind === "vite" ? createRequire(resolve(directory, "package.json")).resolve("vite") : "";
  const run = logged(process.execPath, [fileURLToPath(new URL("./proof-host.js", import.meta.url)), kind, directory, ports, vite], { ...options, env, detached: process.platform !== "win32", livenessPipe: true });
  try {
    const url = await ready(run, line => {
      const match = line.match(/^SNAPBACK4_PROOF_READY (\{.*\})$/);
      if (!match) return;
      const data = JSON.parse(match[1]!);
      if (data.pid !== run.child.pid || data.kind !== kind) throw Error("proof frontend PID mismatch");
      return data.url as string;
    }, timeout);
    const response = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(timeout) });
    if (response.headers.get("x-snapback4-proof") !== String(run.child.pid) + ":" + run.id) throw Error("proof frontend identity mismatch");
    await response.body?.cancel();
    return { url, pid: run.child.pid!, logs: run.logs, stop: () => stopChild(run.child, deadline(options.stopTimeoutMs, 5000)) };
  } catch (error) { await stopChild(run.child, deadline(options.stopTimeoutMs, 5000)); throw error; }
}
/** @throws missing export/index or build script precondition; exhausted ports, readiness deadline or served identity mismatch. */
export function startStatic(dir: string, options: ProofOptions = {}): Promise<ProofHost> { return frontend("static", dir, options); }
/** @throws missing Vite dependency/configuration; exhausted ports, readiness deadline or served identity mismatch. */
export function startVite(dir: string, options: ProofOptions = {}): Promise<ProofHost> { return frontend("vite", dir, options); }

export interface CliResult { code: number | null; signal: NodeJS.Signals | null; stdout: string; stderr: string; logs: ProofHost["logs"] }
/** Waits for close (stdio drained), never merges the two streams. Nonzero is returned for refusal assertions. */
/** @throws spawn error or command deadline; nonzero exit is returned in code, not thrown. */
export function cli(args: readonly string[], options: ProofOptions = {}): Promise<CliResult> {
  return command(process.execPath, [fileURLToPath(new URL("../bin/snapback4.js", import.meta.url)), ...args], { ...options, secret: options.secret || args[0] === "token" });
}
/** A bounded directly spawned build/tool command, with the same file logs. */
/** @throws spawn error or command deadline; nonzero exit is returned in code, not thrown. */
export async function command(binary: string, args: readonly string[], options: ProofOptions = {}): Promise<CliResult> {
  const run = logged(binary, args, { ...options, env: { ...process.env, ...options.env } });
  try {
    await bounded(run.closed, deadline(options.timeoutMs, 120_000), "proof cli close");
    if (run.error()) throw run.error();
    return { code: run.child.exitCode, signal: run.child.signalCode, stdout: run.stdout(), stderr: logText(run.logs.stderr), logs: run.logs };
  } catch (error) { await stopChild(run.child, deadline(options.stopTimeoutMs, 5000)); throw error; }
  finally { run.child.stdout?.destroy(); } // Secret capture must not pin the driver after a deadline.
}
async function successful(args: string[], options: ProofOptions) {
  const result = await cli(args, options);
  if (result.code !== 0) {
    const stdout = result.stdout.trimEnd();
    const tail = stdout.length > 4000 ? `…${stdout.slice(-4000)}` : stdout;
    throw Error(`proof CLI failed (${result.code}): ${result.stderr}; stdout tail: ${tail || "(empty)"}; logs: ${result.logs.stderr}`);
  }
  return result;
}
/** @throws missing explicit saved source, CLI failure, spawn error or deadline. */
export function deploy(dir: string, schema: string, options: ProofOptions = {}) {
  if (typeof schema !== "string" || !schema) throw Error(`proof deploy requires an explicit saved schema file or directory; got ${JSON.stringify(schema) ?? String(schema)}: did npm run day2:proof pass versions/v1?`);
  return successful(["deploy", dir, "--schema", schema, ...(options.args ?? [])], options);
}
/** @throws invalid explicit release target, CLI failure, spawn error or deadline. */
export function rollback(dir: string, to: number | "latest", options: ProofOptions = {}) {
  if (to !== "latest" && (!Number.isSafeInteger(to) || to < 1)) throw Error(`proof rollback requires an explicit immutable release target; got ${JSON.stringify(to) ?? String(to)}`);
  return successful(["rollback", dir, "--to", String(to), ...(options.args ?? [])], options);
}
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#11-wave-14-truthful-starter-custody-day4 — saved-source proof results expose authored cases separately from query sweeps.
export interface TestSourceOptions extends ProofOptions { fixtureOnly?: boolean }
export interface TestSourceResult extends CliResult { casesRun: number; casesSkipped: number }
/** @throws missing source, CLI failure, invalid business-case summary or zero cases without fixtureOnly. */
export function testSource(source: string, options: TestSourceOptions = {}): Promise<TestSourceResult> {
  if (typeof source !== "string" || !source) throw Error(`proof testSource requires an explicit saved source file or directory; got ${JSON.stringify(source) ?? String(source)}: pass versions/v1`);
  const { fixtureOnly = false, ...proofOptions } = options;
  return successful(["test", "--source", source, ...(fixtureOnly ? ["--fixture-only"] : []), ...(proofOptions.args ?? [])], proofOptions).then(result => {
    const summaries = [...result.stdout.matchAll(/^SNAPBACK4_TEST_CASES (\{.*\})$/gm)];
    if (summaries.length !== 1) throw Error(`proof testSource received no unique business-case summary; stdout tail: ${result.stdout.trimEnd().slice(-4000) || "(empty)"}; logs: ${result.logs.stdout}`);
    let cases: { run: number; skipped: number };
    try { cases = JSON.parse(summaries[0]![1]!) as { run: number; skipped: number }; }
    catch { throw Error(`proof testSource received a malformed business-case summary: ${summaries[0]![0]}; logs: ${result.logs.stdout}`); }
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(cases.run) || cases.run < 0 || !Number.isSafeInteger(cases.skipped) || cases.skipped < 0) {
      throw Error(`proof testSource received invalid business-case counts: ${summaries[0]![0]}; logs: ${result.logs.stdout}`);
    }
    if (cases.run === 0 && !fixtureOnly) {
      throw Error(`proof testSource ran no business cases (${cases.skipped} skipped); add test.cases.jsonl for this saved version, or pass { fixtureOnly: true } when fixture loading alone is the intended proof; stdout tail: ${result.stdout.trimEnd().slice(-4000) || "(empty)"}`);
    }
    return { ...result, casesRun: cases.run, casesSkipped: cases.skipped };
  });
}

/** @throws None; returns a boolean for recognized sandbox launch errors. */
export function sandboxUnavailable(error: unknown): boolean {
  return /No usable sandbox|Running as root without --no-sandbox|Failed to move to new namespace|SUID sandbox|sandbox.*Operation not permitted|Failed to unshare.*Operation not permitted/i.test(String(error));
}
/** @throws E_PROOF_BROWSER for missing playwright-core; invalid deadline, browser launch failure or callback error; bounded context/browser cleanup failure. */
export async function withBrowser<T>(options: LaunchOptions & Pick<ProofOptions, "cwd" | "logsDir"> & { chromium?: BrowserType }, proof: (context: BrowserContext) => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
  const { chromium: suppliedChromium, executablePath, cwd: _cwd, logsDir: _logs, ...other } = options;
  const requireCaller = createRequire(resolve(options.cwd ?? process.cwd(), "package.json"));
  let caller = suppliedChromium;
  if (!caller) {
    for (const peer of ["playwright-core", "@playwright/test", "playwright"]) {
      let path: string;
      try { path = requireCaller.resolve(peer); }
      catch (error) { if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code === "MODULE_NOT_FOUND") continue; throw error; }
      const peerModule = await import(path);
      caller = (peerModule.chromium ?? peerModule.default?.chromium) as BrowserType;
      break;
    }
  }
  if (!caller) {
    try { caller = (await import("playwright-core")).chromium; }
    catch (cause) { throw new Error("E_PROOF_BROWSER: withBrowser requires playwright-core; run npm install --save-dev playwright-core before deploying or starting proof hosts", { cause }); }
  }
  const directory = logsDir(options); mkdirSync(directory, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
  const err = openSync(join(directory, `${randomUUID()}.browser.stderr.log`), "wx", 0o600);
  const diagnostic = openSync(join(directory, `${randomUUID()}.witness.private.jsonl`), "wx", 0o600);

  const launch = { ...other, ...(executablePath === undefined ? {} : { executablePath }), timeout: deadline(options.timeout, 30_000), logger: {
    isEnabled: () => true,
    log: (name: string, severity: string, message: string | Error) => { writeSync(err, `${name} ${severity}: ${String(message)}\n`); },
  } };
  const args = [...(options.args ?? [])];
  if (process.getuid?.() === 0 && !args.includes("--no-sandbox")) args.push("--no-sandbox");
  // Playwright owns its browser child over a pipe, including parent-exit cleanup.
  // No WebSocket server or TCP debugging port is opened.
  let browser;
  try {
    try { browser = await caller.launch({ ...launch, args, chromiumSandbox: !args.includes("--no-sandbox") }); }
    catch (error) {
      if (args.includes("--no-sandbox") || !sandboxUnavailable(error)) throw error;
      browser = await caller.launch({ ...launch, args: [...args, "--no-sandbox"], chromiumSandbox: false });
    }
    let context: BrowserContext | undefined;
    try {
      context = await browser.newContext();
      await context.exposeBinding("__snapback4ProofDiagnostic", (_source, value: unknown) => {
        writeSync(diagnostic, `${JSON.stringify(value)}\n`);
      });
      // Init scripts run on each document, including HTTP redirect destinations,
      // before any application script samples its URL.
      await context.addInitScript(() => {
        if (window.top !== window || !/^https?:$/.test(location.protocol)) return;
        const url = new URL(location.href);
        url.searchParams.set("witness", "web");
        history.replaceState(history.state, "", url.href);
      });
      context.setDefaultTimeout(30_000); context.setDefaultNavigationTimeout(30_000);
      return await proof(context);
    } finally {
      try { if (context) await bounded(context.close(), 5000, "browser context close"); }
      finally { await bounded(browser.close(), 5000, "browser close"); }
    }
  } finally { closeSync(err); closeSync(diagnostic); }
}
const substringLocators = new WeakMap<Locator, { locator: Locator; scoped: boolean }>();
/** Strings match exactly by default; use exact:false or a RegExp for a row with more text. Excludes editable values. */
/** @throws None at construction; locator observation can refuse ambiguous or invalid selectors. */
export function renderedText(page: Page, text: string | RegExp, options: { within?: Locator; exact?: boolean } = {}): Locator {
  const root = options.within ?? page, rendered = page.locator(":not(input):not(textarea)");
  const locator = root.getByText(text, { exact: options.exact ?? true }).and(rendered);
  if (typeof text === "string" && options.exact !== false) substringLocators.set(locator, { locator: root.getByText(text, { exact: false }).and(rendered), scoped: options.within !== undefined });
  return locator;
}
/** Activate before taking a screenshot, particularly on loaded shared hosts. */
/** @throws page closed or browser activation failed. */
export async function frontPage(page: Page): Promise<Page> { await page.bringToFront(); return page; }
export { logsDir } from "./proof-process.ts";
/** Returns the fresh nonempty text as a string, after it differs from previous. */
/** @throws observation deadline with scoped locator, last text and visible headings; selector ambiguity is propagated. */
export async function waitForFresh(locator: Locator, previous: string | null, options: number | { within?: Locator; timeoutMs?: number } = 30_000): Promise<string> {
  const timeoutMs = deadline(typeof options === "number" ? options : options.timeoutMs, 30_000);
  let substring = substringLocators.get(locator);
  if (typeof options !== "number" && options.within) {
    locator = options.within.locator(locator);
    if (substring) substring = { locator: options.within.locator(substring.locator), scoped: true };
  }
  const end = performance.now() + timeoutMs;
  let count = 0, text: string | null = null, detail = "";
  while (performance.now() < end) {
    try {
      count = await bounded(locator.count(), Math.max(1, end - performance.now()), "locator count");
      text = count ? await bounded(locator.textContent({ timeout: Math.max(1, Math.min(1000, end - performance.now())) }), Math.max(1, end - performance.now()), "locator text") : null;
      if (text && text !== previous) return text;
    } catch (error) {
      detail = error instanceof Error && (error.name === "TimeoutError" || /timed out after/.test(error.message))
        ? "locator observation exceeded the remaining proof deadline" : String(error);
      // A strictness/selector error needs its original repair, not a timeout.
      if (error instanceof Error && error.name !== "TimeoutError" && !/timed out after/.test(error.message)) throw error;
    }
    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, Math.min(25, Math.max(1, end - performance.now()))));
  }
  const hint = count === 0 && substring?.scoped && await bounded(substring.locator.count(), 1000, "substring count").catch(() => 0)
    ? "; a substring match exists; pass a RegExp or `exact: false`" : "";
  const headings = await bounded(Promise.resolve().then(() => locator.page().locator('h1:visible, h2:visible, [role="heading"]:visible').allTextContents()), 1000, "visible headings").catch(() => []);
  const heading = headings.map(text => text.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim()).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 3).join(" | ").slice(0, 300);
  throw Error(`no fresh signal after ${timeoutMs} ms: ${String(locator)}; last count=${count}, text=${JSON.stringify(text)}; ${count ? "present but unchanged" : "absent"}; previous=${JSON.stringify(previous)}; visible heading=${JSON.stringify(heading || "(none)")}${hint}${detail ? `; ${detail}` : ""}`);
}
/** Poll authoritative server truth after a receipt; a pending prediction is not a commit. */
/** @throws deadline naming label and last value; read or predicate errors propagate. */
export async function waitForServer<T>(read: () => Promise<T>, accepts: (value: T) => boolean, options: { timeoutMs?: number; label?: string } = {}): Promise<T> {
  const timeoutMs = deadline(options.timeoutMs, 30_000), end = performance.now() + timeoutMs;
  let last: T | undefined;
  while (performance.now() < end) {
    let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
    try {
      const value = await Promise.race([Promise.resolve().then(read), new Promise<never>((_, reject) => {
        timer = setTimeout(() => reject(Error(`${options.label ?? "server truth"} timed out after ${timeoutMs} ms; last=${JSON.stringify(last)}`)), Math.max(1, end - performance.now()));
      })]);
      last = value;
      if (accepts(value)) return value;
    } finally { clearTimeout(timer); }
    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, Math.min(25, Math.max(1, end - performance.now()))));
  }
  throw Error(`${options.label ?? "server truth"} timed out after ${timeoutMs} ms; last=${JSON.stringify(last)}`);
}
/** Wait until the page's own client has sampled an advanced server clock. Does not advance or replace that clock. */
/** @throws E_PROOF_USAGE for invalid advanced time; missing/disabled witness, signed-out client, page error or page-clock deadline. */
export async function waitForClock(page: Page, advancedNow: number, options: { timeoutMs?: number } = {}): Promise<number> {
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(advancedNow) || advancedNow < 0) throw Error("E_PROOF_USAGE: waitForClock requires the clock advance reply's now");
  return waitForServer(() => page.evaluate(() => {
    const witness = (window as unknown as { __snapback4Witness?: { clock(): number } }).__snapback4Witness;
    if (!witness?.clock) throw Error("E_WITNESS_DISABLED: registerWebWitness and use witness=web before waitForClock");
    return witness.clock();
  }), now => now >= advancedNow, { ...options, label: "page client clock" });
}
/** @throws locator ambiguity, disabled/closed control, or Playwright deadline. */
export async function fillRN(locator: Locator, text: string): Promise<void> {
  await locator.focus(); await locator.press("ControlOrMeta+A"); await locator.press("Backspace");
  await locator.pressSequentially(text);
}
/** @throws None at construction; ambiguous text is refused when observed. */
export function statusText(page: Page, text: string | RegExp = /^(?:Ready|pending|sent|failed)$/): Locator { return page.getByText(text, { exact: true }); }
/** @throws locator ambiguity, unavailable option or Playwright deadline. */
export async function selectByValue(locator: Locator, value: string): Promise<void> { await locator.selectOption({ value }); }

     1	//! Release row lifetimes whose present values cannot inhabit the selected type.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#91-per-row-narrowed-value-dormancy — hide a reference-closed population; never coerce values.
     3	use super::*;
     4	use crate::schema::{ColumnType, Constraint};
     5	
     6	pub(super) fn present(conn: &Connection) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
     7	    Ok(conn.query_row(
     8	        "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name='_dormant_narrow_rows')",
     9	        [],
    10	        |r| r.get(0),
    11	    )?)
    12	}
    13	
    14	fn incompatible(tx:&Tx<'_>,schema: &Schema, name: &str, row: &Value) -> Result<bool,RuntimeError> {
    15	    incompatible_except(tx,schema,name,row,&[])
    16	}
    17	fn incompatible_except(tx:&Tx<'_>,schema: &Schema,name:&str,row:&Value,authority:&[String])->Result<bool,RuntimeError> {
    18	    let table = &schema.tables[name];
    19	    for (column,kind) in &table.columns {
    20	        if table.engine_field(column) || table.maintained.contains_key(column) || authority.contains(column) {continue;}
    21	        if let Ok(value)=row.field(column) {
    22	            if incompatible_column(tx, table, name, column, kind, value)? { return Ok(true); }
    23	        }
    24	    }
    25	    Ok(false)
    26	}
    27	
    28	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#11-wave-11-factual-release-narration — the warning and activation share the value predicate, including ledger-backed assets.
    29	pub(super) fn incompatible_column(tx: &Tx<'_>, table: &Table, name: &str, column: &str, kind: &ColumnType, value: &Value) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    30	    if crate::engine::validate::validate_column(name, column, kind, Some(value)).is_err() { return Ok(true); }
    31	    // @ref LLP 3000.038#1-the-type — asset facts belong to the ledger, including optional/list leaves.
    32	    match super::activation::validate_assets(tx, kind, Some(value)) {
    33	        Err(RuntimeError::Asset { code: "E_ASSET_TYPE" | "E_ASSET_TOO_LARGE", .. }) => return Ok(true),
    34	        Err(error) => return Err(error),
    35	        Ok(()) => (),
    36	    }
    37	    Ok(table.constraints.iter().any(|c| matches!(c, Constraint::Text { field, min, format }
    38	        if field == column && matches!(value, Value::String(s)
    39	            if crate::engine::validate::validate_text(field, s, *min, *format).is_err()))))
    40	}
    41	
    42	fn refs(kind: &ColumnType, value: &Value, out: &mut Vec<(String, String)>) {
    43	    match (kind, value) {
    44	        (ColumnType::Optional(inner), v) => refs(inner, v, out),
    45	        (ColumnType::List { of, .. } | ColumnType::ListRange { of, .. }, Value::Array(vs)) => {
    46	            for v in vs {
    47	                refs(of, v, out);
    48	            }
    49	        }
    50	        (ColumnType::Ref { table }, Value::Id(id)) => out.push((table.clone(), id.clone())),
    51	        _ => {}
    52	    }
    53	}
    54	
    55	pub(super) fn transition(
    56	    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    57	    next: &Schema,
    58	    previous: Option<&Schema>,
    59	    now: i64,
    60	    restored_images: bool,
    61	    source_definitions: &BTreeMap<String, Table>,
    62	    authority_sources: &BTreeMap<String, Table>,
    63	) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    64	    retire_sources(tx,next,previous,now)?;
    65	    let have_dormant: bool = tx.conn().query_row(
    66	        "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows)",
    67	        [],
    68	        |r| r.get(0),
    69	    )?;
    70	    let type_change = previous.is_some_and(|old| {
    71	        next.tables.iter().any(|(name, t)| {
    72	            old.table(name).is_some_and(|p| {
    73	                t.columns
    74	                    .iter()
    75	                    .any(|(c, k)| p.columns.get(c).is_some_and(|old| old != k))
    76	                    || t.constraints
    77	                        .iter()
    78	                        .filter(|c| matches!(c, Constraint::Text { .. }))
    79	                        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
    80	                        != p.constraints
    81	                            .iter()
    82	                            .filter(|c| matches!(c, Constraint::Text { .. }))
    83	                            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
    84	            })
    85	        })
    86	    });
    87	    if !have_dormant && !type_change && !restored_images {
    88	        return Ok(());
    89	    }
    90	    let mut newly_retained = 0u64;
    91	    // Stream on the primary key. Do not retain a store-sized list of images.
    92	    restore_images(tx,next,now,source_definitions,authority_sources,false)?;
    93	    tx.clear_activation_scan();
    94	    // References to a hidden row hide their dependents, including memberships.
    95	    loop {
    96	        let mut removed = 0u64;
    97	        for (name, table) in next
    98	            .tables
    99	            .iter()
   100	            .filter(|(n, t)| t.ephemeral.is_none() && next.maintain_for(n).is_none())
   101	        {
   102	            let t = tx.num(name)?;
   103	            super::activation::walk(tx, t, |id, mut row| {
   104	                if let Some(source)=authority_sources.get(name) {
   105	                    if crate::engine::through_activation::Retirement::retires_image(table,source,&row,now) {
   106	                        tx.delete(name,source,&row)?;
   107	                        return Ok(());
   108	                    }
   109	                }
   110	                crate::schema::materialize_optional_fields(
   111	                    &mut row,
   112	                    previous.and_then(|s| s.table(name)).unwrap_or(table),
   113	                );
   114	                let mut hidden = incompatible(tx,next, name, &row)?;
   115	                for (column, kind) in &table.columns {
   116	                    let mut edges = Vec::new();
   117	                    if let Ok(value) = row.field(column) {
   118	                        refs(kind, value, &mut edges);
   119	                    }
   120	                    for (parent, parent_id) in edges {
   121	                        let missing = next.table(&parent).is_none()
   122	                            || (!tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2)",params![tx.num(&parent)?,parent_id],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))?
   123	                                && reserved(tx, tx.num(&parent)?, &parent_id)?);
   124	                        if missing {
   125	                            hidden = true;
   126	                        }
   127	                    }
   128	                }
   129	                if !hidden {
   130	                    return Ok(());
   131	                }
   132	                let retained: bool = tx.conn().query_row(
   133	                    "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2)",
   134	                    params![t, id],
   135	                    |r| r.get(0),
   136	                )?;
   137	                if !retained {
   138	                    newly_retained += 1;
   139	                    let old = authority_sources.get(name).unwrap_or(table);
   140	                    tx.conn().execute(
   141	                        "INSERT INTO _dormant_narrow_rows VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5)",
   142	                        params![
   143	                            t,
   144	                            id,
   145	                            canonical_json(&row),
   146	                            canonical_json(old),
   147	                            "type or dormant reference"
   148	                        ],
   149	                    )?;
   150	                    for (column, kind) in &old.columns {
   151	                        // Retained cells/whole rows already captured a lifetime; moving them must not erase deletion evidence.
   152	                        let captured:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3)",params![t,id,column],|r|r.get(0))?;
   153	                        if captured {
   154	                            continue;
   155	                        }
   156	                        if let Ok(value) = row.field(column) {
   157	                            if !super::authority_origin::live_input(old,column) {super::dormant::capture_refs(tx, t, id, column, kind, value)?;}
   158	                        }
   159	                        super::dormant::capture_through_parent(tx, name, old, id, column, &row)?;
   160	                        // Nominal keep references may already be dangling. They have no bound lifetime.
   161	                    }
   162	                }
   163	                tx.conn()
   164	                    .execute("DELETE FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2", params![t, id])?;
   165	                removed += 1;
   166	                Ok(())
   167	            })?;
   168	            tx.clear_activation_scan();
   169	        }
   170	        if removed == 0 {
   171	            break;
   172	        }
   173	    }
   174	    // Consume only rows that survived the closed population, not hidden dependents.
   175	    let restored:u64=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=n.t AND r.id=n.id)",[],|r|r.get::<_,i64>(0).map(|n|n as u64))?;
   176	    if super::live_activation::active(tx)? {
   177	        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._live_restored SELECT 'narrowed',n.t,'',n.id FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=n.t AND r.id=n.id)",[])?;
   178	    } else {
   179	    let restored_rows=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT c.name,n.id FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n JOIN _catalog c ON c.num=n.t WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=n.t AND r.id=n.id)")?.query_map([],|r|Ok((r.get::<_,String>(0)?,r.get::<_,String>(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?;
   180	    let mut restored_tables:std::collections::BTreeMap<String,Vec<String>>=std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
   181	    for (name,id) in restored_rows {restored_tables.entry(name).or_default().push(id);}
   182	    for (name, ids) in restored_tables {
   183	        tx.set_meta(&format!("activation_narrowed_restored:{name}"),&ids.len().to_string())?;
   184	        tx.set_meta(&format!("activation_narrowed_restored_ids:{name}"),&serde_json::to_string(&ids).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?)?;
   185	    }
   186	    }
   187	    tx.set_meta("activation_narrowed_retained", &newly_retained.to_string())?;
   188	    tx.set_meta("activation_narrowed_restored", &restored.to_string())?;
   189	    tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=_dormant_narrow_rows.t AND r.id=_dormant_narrow_rows.id)",[])?;
   190	    if newly_retained > 0 || restored > 0 {
   191	        tx.set_meta("activation_population", "true")?;
   192	    }
   193	    Ok(())
   194	}
   195	
   196	pub(super) fn reserved(tx: &Tx<'_>, t: i64, id: &str) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
   197	    if !present(tx.conn())? {
   198	        return Ok(false);
   199	    }
   200	    Ok(tx.conn().query_row(
   201	        "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2)",
   202	        params![t, id],
   203	        |r| r.get(0),
   204	    )?)
   205	}
   206	
   207	pub(super) fn check_write(
   208	    tx: &Tx<'_>,
   209	    name: &str,
   210	    _table: &Table,
   211	    row: &Value,
   212	    old: Option<&Value>,
   213	) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   214	    if !present(tx.conn())? {
   215	        return Ok(());
   216	    }
   217	    let id = row.field("id")?.as_str()?;
   218	    let conflict = old.is_none() && reserved(tx, tx.num(name)?, id)?;
   219	    if conflict {
   220	        return Err(RuntimeError::Rule {
   221	            table: name.into(),
   222	            effect: if old.is_some() {
   223	                crate::schema::Effect::Update
   224	            } else {
   225	                crate::schema::Effect::Insert
   226	            },
   227	        });
   228	    }
   229	    Ok(())
   230	}
   231	
   232	pub(super) fn retire_sources(tx:&Tx<'_>,next:&Schema,previous:Option<&Schema>,now:i64)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   233	    // An expired predecessor must not be archived and later revived without its lifetime.
   234	    if let Some(previous) = previous {
   235	        let retirement = crate::engine::through_activation::Retirement::new(next, previous, now);
   236	        for (name, old) in &previous.tables {
   237	            if next.tables.contains_key(name) && retirement.affects(name) {
   238	                super::activation::walk(tx, tx.num(name)?, |_, row| {
   239	                    if retirement.retires(name, &row) {
   240	                        tx.delete(name, old, &row)?;
   241	                    }
   242	                    Ok(())
   243	                })?;
   244	            }
   245	        }
   246	    }
   247	    Ok(())
   248	}
   249	
   250	// Restore retained source images before backfill evaluation. Candidate-owned
   251	// output cells are checked after transformation, so a same-column enum rename
   252	// does not preclude its own source image from entering the snapshot.
   253	pub(super) fn restore_images(tx:&Tx<'_>,next:&Schema,now:i64,source_definitions:&BTreeMap<String,Table>,authority_sources:&BTreeMap<String,Table>,for_backfills:bool)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   254	    let rewritten=if for_backfills {super::backfills::columns(tx,next)?}else{Default::default()};
   255	    let mut cursor = (0i64, String::new());
   256	    loop {
   257	        let item: Option<(i64,String,i64,i64)> = tx.conn().query_row(
   258	            "SELECT t,id,octet_length(data),octet_length(definition) FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE (t,id)>(?1,?2) AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=_dormant_narrow_rows.t AND r.id=_dormant_narrow_rows.id) ORDER BY t,id LIMIT 1",
   259	            params![cursor.0,cursor.1],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?,r.get(2)?,r.get(3)?))).optional()?;
   260	        let Some((t,id,data_bytes,definition_bytes))=item else {break};
   261	        // Retain one admitted provenance/image unit through decode, overlays,
   262	        // reconciliation and encoding. Never own its blobs before this bound.
   263	        let mut admission=crate::meter::Meter::new(crate::meter::Ceilings::job(),crate::meter::Cut::Abort);
   264	        if super::live_activation::active(tx)? {
   265	            admission.heap((data_bytes as u64).saturating_add(definition_bytes as u64).saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(8192))?;
   266	        }
   267	        let (data,definition):(String,String)=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data,definition FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?)))?;
   268	        cursor = (t, id.clone());
   269	        let name: String =
   270	            tx.conn()
   271	                .query_row("SELECT name FROM _catalog WHERE num=?1", [t], |r| r.get(0))?;
   272	        let Some(table) = next
   273	            .table(&name)
   274	            .filter(|t| t.ephemeral.is_none() && next.maintain_for(&name).is_none())
   275	        else {
   276	            continue;
   277	        };
   278	        let old: Table =
   279	            serde_json::from_str(&definition).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
   280	        super::dormant::check_owner(tx, &name, &old, next)?;
   281	        let mut row: Value =
   282	            serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
   283	        crate::schema::materialize_optional_fields(&mut row,&old);
   284	        let mut authority=crate::engine::through_activation::manual_authority_fields(next,&name,&old);
   285	        if for_backfills { authority.extend(rewritten.get(&name).into_iter().flatten().cloned()); }
   286	        if incompatible_except(tx,next,&name,&row,&authority)? {
   287	            continue;
   288	        }
   289	        // Retained source deadlines precede projection, including removal of read-through itself.
   290	        if crate::engine::through_activation::Retirement::retires_image(table,&old,&row,now) {
   291	            tx.delete(&name,&old,&row)?;
   292	            continue;
   293	        }
   294	        // Actual deletion/reuse never recaptures a different parent lifetime.
   295	        let invalid_columns = tx
   296	            .conn()
   297	            .prepare_cached(
   298	                "SELECT column_name FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND invalid=1",
   299	            )?
   300	            .query_map(params![t, id], |r| r.get::<_, String>(0))?
   301	            .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;
   302	        let mut invalid=false;
   303	        for c in &invalid_columns {
   304	            if table.columns.contains_key(c) && !super::authority_origin::obsolete_ref(tx,t,&id,c,table,Some(&old))? {invalid=true;}
   305	        }
   306	        if invalid {
   307	            continue;
   308	        }
   309	        if super::authority_origin::live_row(tx,t,&id,&old,table)? {
   310	            admission.heap(crate::engine::through_activation::restored_parent_scratch(next,source_definitions,&name,&old).saturating_add(crate::rows::disclosed::decode_reservation(table).saturating_mul(4)))?;
   311	        }
   312	        super::authority_origin::record(tx,t,&id,&old)?;
   313	        super::authority_origin::validate(tx,next,authority_sources,&name,&old,&row,now,true)?;
   314	        crate::engine::through_activation::forward_image(tx,next,authority_sources,&name,&old,&row,now)?;
   315	        if old.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|e|e.live.is_some()) {tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._live_restored(kind,t,col,id) VALUES('live-authority',?1,'',?2)",params![t,id])?;}
   316	        for column in old.columns.keys() {
   317	            if !table.columns.contains_key(column) {
   318	                super::dormant::definition(tx, &name, &old, column)?;
   319	                if let Ok(value) = row.field(column) {
   320	                    super::dormant::save_cell(tx, &name, &old, &id, column, value, false)?;
   321	                }
   322	            }
   323	        }
   324	        if !for_backfills { if let Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) = &mut row {
   325	            fields.retain(|c, _| table.columns.contains_key(c));
   326	        }}
   327	        let values=super::dormant::restored_values(tx,t,&id,table,&mut admission)?;
   328	        super::authority_origin::overlay(tx,t,&id,Some(&old),table,&mut row,values)?;
   329	        if old.through.as_ref().is_none_or(|e|e.live.is_some()) {
   330	            for column in table.columns.keys() {super::dormant::mark_live_column(tx,t,&id,column)?;}
   331	        }
   332	        crate::engine::through_activation::manual_image(tx,next,&name,&old,&mut row,now)?;
   333	        if if for_backfills {incompatible_except(tx,next,&name,&row,&authority)?}else{incompatible(tx,next,&name,&row)?} {continue;}
   334	        if !for_backfills {crate::schema::materialize_optional_fields(&mut row, table);}
   335	        // Missing required new fields are a genuine backfill refusal, not dormancy.
   336	        tx.conn().execute(
   337	            "INSERT INTO r(t,id,data) VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",
   338	            params![t, id, canonical_json(&row)],
   339	        )?;
   340	    }
   341	    tx.clear_activation_scan();
   342	    Ok(())
   343	}
