# Deploy: upgrade, real accounts and schema evolution

A release is immutable source over one durable store. JSON fields, bounded ready waits and worker roots: `guide release-json`. Deploy from the app directory:

```sh
bunx snapback4 check
bunx snapback4 test
bunx snapback4 deploy ./deployment
bunx snapback4 serve ./deployment --port 4400
```
For a complete real-user staging script and first-admin setup, run `guide staging`. Bulk account setup is restricted to fresh `deploy --staging` stores. Generations climb on every actual switch, including rollback and forward; selecting identical execution content is a no-op. `token --service ops --release ./deployment` works while serve runs. Signups are rate-limited: create proof accounts sequentially and respect a returned retry delay. Time-gated mutations on the real clock need a bounded retry of the authored refusal (for example `NOT_OPEN`), with a deadline and short delay; never retry unrelated refusals. Development and classified staging proofs can advance the clock (`guide time`); production always uses wall time.

## Upgrade
`snapback4 upgrade [version]` resolves once and exactly pins core and installed Expo adapters with npm/bun/pnpm. Rebuild native apps after adapter changes; commit the lockfile, run check/typecheck/acceptance. `test` uses ./fixtures; `--fixture dir` overrides it. Use the password and first-admin recipes below.

## Deploy and password identity
Add `use identity password` (included below). Deploy from the app directory onto durable storage:
Serve admits bearer sessions; personas are 401 and /seed/dev doors 403, even through proxies. Signup creates an account, not a privileged role. See guide client for SDK session stores; auth/headless for account setup. HTTP auth returns session/denied; SDK signup/login from `snapback4/auth` returns ok/why. Check warns on installed version skew.
```sh
U=http://127.0.0.1:4400
curl -s "$U/auth/signup" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -d '{"email":"admin@example.test","password":"replace-with-a-private-password"}'
# {session:{principal,kind,token,expiresAt,scope}}
curl -s "$U/auth/login" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -d '{"email":"admin@example.test","password":"replace-with-a-private-password"}'
```
Keep the session and send `Authorization: Bearer <token>`. Wrong credentials or a disabled identity method are 401 E_AUTH. Sessions survive ordinary deployments and restarts until expiry/revocation; a replaced/reset store requires sign-in again. Never delete a store to make adoption pass. `serve --port N` accepts 0 or A-B; stdout's first line is its URL, stderr includes SNAPBACK4_READY. Wait for readiness.

Layout: releases/N/ holds source; store.sqlite data/sessions/queues; assets/ uploads. The database owns current release/history; current is a text convenience copy, releases.jsonl a convenience ledger. Back up the whole directory, including sidecars/assets/releases, with serve and external workers stopped (CLIENT-AND-OPERATIONS.md §6). A restore does not rewind external effects.

## First admin: a service seat
Provision roles through an operator seat, with no public first-user race:
```quarry
use identity password
table admins:
  principal: principal
  unique principal
  immutable principal
  sync
  read <- .principal = viewer or viewer = 'service:ops'
  insert <- viewer = 'service:ops' or exists admins[principal = viewer]
mutation grantAdmin(principal: principal):
  return insert admins { principal }
table notes:
  owner: principal
  body: text <=200
  archived: bool default false
  status: enum('draft', 'live') default 'draft'
  read <- .owner = viewer
  insert <- .owner = viewer
```
```sh
TOKEN=$(bunx snapback4 token --service ops --release ./deployment)
curl -s "$U/m/grantAdmin" -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"args":{"principal":"user:SIGNED_UP_ID"}}'
unset TOKEN
```
This roster is synced. A whole service-seat branch may add the first member; ordinary callers must already hold the roster. Service grants add no device audience. The seat's own program lookups and `exists` obey read rules too: to inspect a roster, add `or viewer = 'service:ops'` to its read rule, as above. A rule probe has authorization visibility; a program probe does not. The mutation completes before the grant syncs; wait for the enrolled account's client to receive its scope before using offline staff reads.

A synced row has at most one group-roster audience: cross-table crew grants are not a second group. Enroll those readers in the same roster or keep the table `online only` (`guide rule-recipes`). Mint locally against the same store. Token stdout is only the credential; prose is stderr. Protect it; it expires under the seat-session policy. Rotate the credential by revoking its session (guide services) and minting a replacement. Expired or revoked bearer sessions cannot invoke the mutation. A repeat grant hits uniqueness; add membership revocation deliberately. Programmatic account sessions use `mintSession(url, serviceToken, principal)` from `snapback4/worker` (guide services); service seats themselves are minted locally by `token`.

## Migrations and adoption
Optional/defaulted columns, new tables/ops, non-unique indexes and wider types are additive. Required new fields need a literal default or a declarative `backfill table name(fields: types): {column: expression}`; default now/viewer cannot invent historical values. Dev check/adoption refuses incompatible values with E_ADOPT. Release type narrowing instead retains each row whose present values cannot fit. Unique/cap conflicts still refuse atomically; repair active rows, or restore through a widened non-unique bridge, repair, then reinstate constraints. Prefer expand → migrate → contract for client compatibility.

## Rollback after widening, restore and prune
```sh
bunx snapback4 rollback ./deployment --to 1 --check
bunx snapback4 rollback ./deployment --to 1
```
`deploy ./deployment --check` previews deploy direction and restoration intent. `bunx snapback4 check --store ./deployment/store.sqlite --release 1` also previews a selection; `bunx snapback4 releases ./deployment latest` inspects it. Forward with `bunx snapback4 rollback ./deployment --to latest`. Preview runs exact adoption on a private disk snapshot and removes it, without activating; disk/time scales with the store, and later writes can change the plan. Release is immutable source; latest is the highest successfully adopted source even after rollback. A refused candidate is never selectable. Bare rollback selects the previously active release; rollback prints the resolved target before preview or activation. Generation is a monotone identity; releases N/latest inspects source. Identical source is a no-op. Deploy/rollback return after serve switches: `release N is current as generation G`. In-flight one-shot operations finish in their generation; no settle sleep is needed. Default adoption is one measured receipt; --verbose/--json includes phases/cache advice. N_ADOPTION_PAUSE appears above one second. A dev/older unreachable owner requires stopping that owned process before retrying; failed adoption keeps current.

Widened enum values, or short codes a loose release wrote, become dormant rows when selected types cannot hold them; compatible forward restores exact values. Reference dependents also leave the active population. Rules, counts, maintained views and reacquired sync partitions see only active rows. Point reads/updates/ deletes see absent rows; dormant ids stay reserved. Nominal reference values stay legal, as for other missing ids; require/read-through decides live-target admission. Dormant rows occupy no cap/unique slots, so older writes can require a bridge repair. Removed tables/columns/indexes also stay dormant. Fresh-source name reuse needs --restore-dormant; rollback selects restoration explicitly. Old active updates keep dormant cells; active deletion removes them. Only active referential actions run: parent deletion invalidates dormant lifetimes. Narrowed rows with invalid visible references stay dormant; whole-table/column restoration retains its E_ADOPT barrier. Prune previews counts/bytes/sample ids; `prune ./deployment --allow-destructive` permanently drops all dormant storage. No age collector or implicit pruning exists. Deploy/rollback --allow-destructive bypasses neither validation nor retention. --allow-feed-adoption permits the explicit feed launch. Inspect releases after an ambiguous reply. Details: [SERVER.md — Release lifecycle](SERVER.md#release-lifecycle).

Check business compatibility separately: old payment code may accept payment while a new review row is dormant. Rollback preview identifies current safety guards/rules and read confidentiality the target loses, active table-population changes, and current → target quotas. Preserve the current domain, audience, guard or quota in a bridge release, or pause that workflow. Old source cannot supply absent product logic. An absent operation returns 404 `{denied:{code:"E_OP",…},generation:G}`; a mutation's incompatible arguments return 200 `{state:"failed",why:{code:"E_INPUT",…},generation:G}`.

## Bridge a safety gate before rollback
Use this beside the enum-widening rollback recipe above. Suppose v2 added `grounding.state = 'inspection'`; v1 holds only `'clear'`/`'hold'`. Selecting v1 hides that row and its children, so v1's `book` guard may allow a booking that v2 blocks. Counts and sample ids are snapshot dependencies, not predicate matches; later writes can change the plan.

1. Start from the old UI/operations, but keep v2's widened `grounding.state` domain, safety table, read audience and booking `require`. Save this complete source as `versions/bridge/schema.q` and deploy it with `deploy ./deployment --schema versions/bridge/schema.q --check`, then without `--check`.
2. While the gate still runs, migrate each inspection to a truthful old-domain hold through an authorized bridge mutation. Do not relabel it clear just to satisfy v1. Keep linked metadata until its parent can be safely represented; if v1 cannot invalidate newer metadata, keep that behavior in the bridge too.
3. Prove the same booking/payment still refuses for the blocked case and that the named lists/counts preserve the intended business denominator. Re-run the final old-source preview against the repaired live population.
4. Select v1 only when those checks pass. If the old source cannot preserve the invariant, keep the bridge or pause that workflow. Retained storage cannot supply absent logic.

## What the old release sees
| Request after rollback | Result |
|---|---|
| An operation absent from the old release | HTTP 404 `{denied:{code:"E_OP"}}` |
| Bare point query of a dormant row | Reads null |
| Point query or mutation with an explicit `! else CODE` guard | Refuses its guard code (`NOT_FOUND` when authored that way) |
| Added columns after rollback | Ignored by the older release; their cells stay dormant |
| Counts, rules, maintained views and sync | Only the active population |
| Compatible forward to the newer release | Original dormant values restored |

This proof assumes v1 declares `readItem(item)` returning `items[item]` and `editItem(item)` with `row = items[item]! else NOT_FOUND`; v2 adds `newOnly` and writes an enum value v1 cannot hold. Keep the bearer session in `headers`:

const fetchIdentity = async (url: string, timeoutMs: number) => {
  const response = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(timeoutMs) });
  return { response, body: await response.json() as Record<string, unknown> };
};
async function nativeHost(verb: "dev" | "serve", prefix: string[], options: ProofOptions): Promise<ProofHost & { personas?: string[] }> {
  const env = { ...process.env, ...options.env }, id = randomUUID();
  const ports = portRange(env), timeout = deadline(options.timeoutMs, 30_000);
  const args = [...(options.args ?? [])];
  if (!args.includes("--port")) args.push("--port", ports);
  const run = logged(resolveBinary(), [verb, ...prefix, ...args], { ...options, env: { ...env, SNAPBACK4_PROOF_ID: id, SNAPBACK4_SUPERVISOR_PID: String(process.pid) } });
  try {
    const url = await ready(run, line => {
      const match = line.match(/^SNAPBACK4_READY verb=(dev|serve) generation=\d+ url=(http:\/\/[^ ]+) pid=(\d+)(?: |$)/);
      if (!match) return;
      if (match[1] !== verb || Number(match[3]) !== run.child.pid) throw Error("proof ready identity mismatch");
      return match[2];
    }, timeout);
    const { response, body } = await fetchIdentity(`${url}/auth/config`, timeout);
    if (!response.ok || body.proof_id !== id) throw Error("proof served identity mismatch");
    let personas: string[] = [];
    if (verb === "dev" && body.devDoors !== false) {
      const status = await fetchIdentity(`${url}/dev/status`, timeout);
      if (status.body.pid !== run.child.pid) throw Error("proof dev PID mismatch");
      if (!Array.isArray(status.body.personas) || !status.body.personas.every(name => typeof name === "string")) throw Error("proof dev personas missing");
      personas = status.body.personas;
    }
    return { url, ...(verb === "dev" ? { personas } : {}), pid: run.child.pid!, logs: run.logs, stop: () => stopChild(run.child, deadline(options.stopTimeoutMs, 5000)) };
  } catch (error) { await stopChild(run.child, deadline(options.stopTimeoutMs, 5000)); throw error; }
}
export interface DevHost extends ProofHost {
  personas: string[];
  /** Read the current authoritative development time, including all clock advances. */
  now(): Promise<number>;
}
/** The array form preserves the former startDev(args) recipe. */
/** @throws invalid port range or conflicting memory flags; child exits, readiness deadline, PID/served identity mismatch, or unavailable dev clock. */
export function startDev(options: ProofOptions | readonly string[] = {}): Promise<DevHost> {
  const opts: ProofOptions = Array.isArray(options) ? { args: options } : options as ProofOptions;
  const args = [...(opts.args ?? [])];
  const persistent = args.some(arg => arg === "--store" || arg.startsWith("--store=") || arg === "--no-memory");
  if (persistent && args.includes("--memory")) throw new TypeError("proof startDev: --memory conflicts with --store/--no-memory");
  // Native persistence is the default. --no-memory remains a consumed explicit alias.
  const host = nativeHost("dev", [], { ...opts, args: args.filter(arg => arg !== "--no-memory") });
  return host.then(host => ({ ...host, personas: host.personas!, now: async () => {
    const { response, body } = await fetchIdentity(`${host.url}/dev/status`, deadline(opts.timeoutMs, 10_000));
    if (!response.ok || typeof body.now !== "number") throw Error("proof dev clock unavailable; password-only dev requires explicit --unsafe-dev-doors for clock/fixture tools; use serve staging for real users");
    return body.now;
  } }));
}

  operatorToken(service?: string, days?: number): Promise<string>;

/** @throws E_PROOF_USAGE for missing deployment directory; child exit, readiness deadline or served identity mismatch. */
export async function startServe(deployDir: string, options: ServeOptions | readonly string[] = {}): Promise<ServeHost> {
  if (typeof deployDir !== "string" || !deployDir.trim()) throw Object.assign(new TypeError("E_PROOF_USAGE: startServe(deployDir, options) requires a deployment directory as its first argument"), { code: "E_PROOF_USAGE" });
  const opts: ServeOptions = Array.isArray(options) ? { args: options } : options as ServeOptions;
  const directory = resolve(opts.cwd ?? ".", deployDir);
  if (opts.clockOffset !== undefined && (typeof opts.clockOffset !== "string" || !/^[+-]?\d+(ms|s|m|h|d|w)$/.test(opts.clockOffset))) throw new TypeError("E_PROOF_USAGE: clockOffset is a signed duration such as +25h");
  if (opts.clockOffset !== undefined && opts.args?.some(arg => arg === "--clock-offset" || arg.startsWith("--clock-offset="))) throw new TypeError("E_PROOF_USAGE: pass clockOffset once");
  const host = await nativeHost("serve", [directory], { ...opts, args: [...(opts.args ?? []), ...(opts.clockOffset === undefined ? [] : ["--clock-offset", opts.clockOffset])] });
  const clockCall = async (body?: object): Promise<number> => {
    const config = await fetchIdentity(`${host.url}/auth/config`, deadline(opts.timeoutMs, 10_000));
    if (!config.response.ok || config.body.environment !== "staging") throw Error("E_CLOCK_DEV_ONLY: clock controls require a deploy --staging store");

"POST" : "GET", headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${await clockToken}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" }, ...(body ? { body: JSON.stringify(body) } : {}), signal: AbortSignal.timeout(deadline(opts.timeoutMs, 60_000)) });
    const result = await response.json() as { now?: number; denied?: { code?: string; message?: string } };
    if (!response.ok || result.denied || !Number.isSafeInteger(result.now)) throw Error(`staging clock refused or reply unavailable; inspect clock and queued work before retrying: ${result.denied?.code ?? response.status} ${result.denied?.message ?? ""}`);
    return result.now!;
  };

const CUE:&str="table cues:\n  status: enum('standby','done')\n  public 'fixture'\n  update <- allow\n  online only\n";
fn cues()->String{CUE.replace("enum('standby','done')","enum('standby','complete','skipped')")+"backfill cues rename(status: enum('standby','done')):\n  {status: 'complete' if status = 'done' else status}\nmutation skip():\n  update cues['a'] {status:'skipped'}\n"}
#[test]
fn enum_rename_reverses_exact_preimage_and_refuses_post_backfill_edits(){
 let mut e=engine(CUE);seed(&mut e,"cues",json!({"id":"a","status":"done"}));adopt(&mut e,&cues(),2).unwrap();
 assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"cues","a","status"),Value::String("complete".into()));
 adopt(&mut e,CUE,1).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"cues","a","status"),Value::String("done".into()));
 adopt(&mut e,&cues(),2).unwrap();write(&mut e,"skip");let g=e.generation();
 let error=adopt(&mut e,CUE,1).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("changed after backfill"),"{error}");assert_eq!(g,e.generation());
 assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"cues","a","status"),Value::String("skipped".into()));
}
#[test]
fn backfill_and_evidence_roll_back_with_later_unique_failure(){

 assert_ne!(e.store().meta("version").unwrap().as_deref(),Some(super::backfills::VERSION));

}

#[test]
fn string_split_and_ascii_digit_classification_are_deterministic(){
 let source="table people:\n  name: text <=100\n  location: text <=100\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=source.replace("  name:","  first: text <=100\n  last: text <=100\n  room: bool\n  name:")+"backfill people parts(name: text <=100, location: text <=100):\n  {first: beforeLast(name, ' '), last: afterLast(name, ' '), room: allDigits(location)}\n";
 let mut e=engine(source);for(id,name,location)in[("a","Ada Lovelace","123"),("b","Prince","１２３"),("c","A B C","")]{seed(&mut e,"people",json!({"id":id,"name":name,"location":location}));}
 adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();
 for(id,first,last,room)in[("a","Ada","Lovelace",true),("b","Prince","",false),("c","A B","C",false)]{assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"people",id,"first"),Value::String(first.into()));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"people",id,"last"),Value::String(last.into()));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"people",id,"room"),Value::Bool(room));}
}

#[test]
fn all_fifty_wave17_change_shapes_run_without_migration_programs(){
 let cases:Vec<Json>=serde_json::from_str(include_str!("../../tests/backfills/wave17.json")).unwrap();assert_eq!(cases.len(),50);
 for case in cases {
  let lane=case["lane"].as_u64().unwrap();let inputs=case["inputs"].as_object().unwrap();let outputs=case["outputs"].as_object().unwrap();
  let columns=|fields:&serde_json::Map<String,Json>|fields.iter().map(|(c,t)|format!("  {c}: {}\n",t.as_str().unwrap())).collect::<String>();
  let old=format!("table rows:\n  legacy: bool default true\n{}  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n",columns(inputs));
  let args=inputs.iter().map(|(c,t)|format!("{c}: {}",t.as_str().unwrap())).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
  let next=format!("table rows:\n  legacy: bool default true\n{}  public 'fixture'\n  online only\nbackfill rows change{lane}({args}):\n  {}\n",columns(outputs),case["expression"].as_str().unwrap());
  let bound=snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),next.clone())]);assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(),"lane {lane} {}: {:?}\n{next}",case["slug"],bound.diagnostics);
  let mut e=engine(&old);
  for (i,r) in case["rows"].as_array().unwrap().iter().enumerate(){let mut input=r["input"].as_object().unwrap().clone();input.insert("id".into(),json!(format!("r{i}")));seed(&mut e,"rows",Json::Object(input));}
  adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap_or_else(|error|panic!("lane {lane}: {error}\n{next}"));
  assert!(e.schema().public_projection().backfills.is_empty(),"historical transforms never enter device input");
  for(i,r)in case["rows"].as_array().unwrap().iter().enumerate(){
   let got=row(&mut e,"rows",&format!("r{i}"));
   for(c,wanted)in r["expect"].as_object().unwrap(){assert_eq!(crate::wire::to_json(got.field(c).unwrap()),*wanted,"lane {lane} row {i} column {c}");}
  }
  adopt(&mut e,&old,1).unwrap_or_else(|error|panic!("lane {lane} rollback: {error}"));
  for(i,r)in case["rows"].as_array().unwrap().iter().enumerate(){for(c,wanted)in r["input"].as_object().unwrap(){assert_eq!(crate::wire::to_json(&field(&mut e,"rows",&format!("r{i}"),c)),*wanted,"lane {lane} restored {c}");}}
  adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap_or_else(|error|panic!("lane {lane} forward: {error}"));
 }
}
#[test]
fn unique_lookup_uses_historical_rows_and_rechecks_changed_lookup_values(){
 let old="table members:\n  principal: principal\n  plot: text <=24\n  unique principal\n  read <- deny\n  online only\ntable bookings:\n  owner: principal\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.replace("  owner: principal","  plot: text <=24\n  owner: principal")+"backfill bookings plot(owner: principal):\n  {plot:(members[principal = owner] ! else NOT_FOUND).plot}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"members",json!({"id":"m","principal":"user:a","plot":"A17"}));seed(&mut e,"bookings",json!({"id":"a","owner":"user:a"}));
 adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"bookings","a","plot"),Value::String("A17".into()));
 adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();
 // Trusted seed replaces the source row as an operator update for this test.
 seed(&mut e,"members",json!({"id":"m","principal":"user:a","plot":"B20"}));
 adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"bookings","a","plot"),Value::String("B20".into()));
}
#[test]
fn cross_reference_existence_is_evaluated_before_any_table_output_changes(){
 let old="table sightings:\n  tower: text <=20\n  reportedAt: time\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.replace("  tower:","  confidence: enum('confirmed','possible')\n  tower:")+"backfill sightings confidence(tower: text <=20, reportedAt: time):\n  {confidence:'confirmed' if exists sightings where .tower != tower and .reportedAt >= reportedAt - 15m and .reportedAt <= reportedAt + 15m else 'possible'}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"sightings",json!({"id":"a","tower":"north","reportedAt":1000000}));seed(&mut e,"sightings",json!({"id":"b","tower":"south","reportedAt":1100000}));seed(&mut e,"sightings",json!({"id":"c","tower":"west","reportedAt":9000000}));
 adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"sightings","a","confidence"),Value::String("confirmed".into()));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"sightings","b","confidence"),Value::String("confirmed".into()));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"sightings","c","confidence"),Value::String("possible".into()));
}
#[test]
fn retained_table_reentry_never_feeds_a_transform_its_own_output(){
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.to_owned()+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {n:n * 2}\n";
 for direct_old in [false,true] {
  let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();
  adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",3).unwrap();
  if direct_old {adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","n"),Value::Int(3));}
  else {adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","n"),Value::Int(6));adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","n"),Value::Int(3));}
 }
}
#[test]
fn previously_narrowed_rows_join_the_backfill_source_snapshot(){
 let old="table facts:\n  principal: principal\n  n: int 0..20\n  unique principal\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\ntable bookings:\n  owner: principal\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let narrow=old.replace("int 0..20","int 0..5");
 let next=old.replace("  n: int","  doubled: int\n  n: int").replace("  owner: principal","  score: int\n  owner: principal")+"backfill facts twice(n: int):\n  {doubled:n * 2}\nbackfill bookings scores(owner: principal):\n  {score:(facts[principal = owner] ! else NOT_FOUND).n}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"facts",json!({"id":"a","principal":"user:a","n":9}));seed(&mut e,"bookings",json!({"id":"b","owner":"user:a"}));
 adopt(&mut e,&narrow,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(e.store().count("facts").unwrap(),0);
 adopt(&mut e,&next,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"facts","a","doubled"),Value::Int(18));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"bookings","b","score"),Value::Int(9));
}
#[test]

#[test]
fn pruning_preimages_is_visible_and_exact_reversal_then_refuses(){
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";let next=old.to_owned()+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {n:n * 2}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();
 let preview=e.store_mut().prune_dormant(false,None).unwrap();assert!(preview["items"].as_array().unwrap().iter().any(|i|i["kind"]=="backfill"&&i["rows"]==1));
 e.store_mut().prune_dormant(true,None).unwrap();let error=adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("explicitly pruned"),"{error}");assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","n"),Value::Int(6));
}
#[test]
fn carried_backfills_apply_to_rows_restored_for_the_first_time(){
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  label: enum('a','b')\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let narrow=old.replace("enum('a','b')","enum('a')");
 let with_fill=narrow.replace("  n: int","  doubled: int\n  n: int")+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {doubled:n * 2}\n";
 let restored=with_fill.replace("enum('a')","enum('a','b')");
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":9,"label":"b"}));
 adopt(&mut e,&narrow,2).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&with_fill,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(e.store().count("items").unwrap(),0);
 adopt(&mut e,&restored,4).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","doubled"),Value::Int(18));
}

codex
The code stages patches before applying them and checks historical lookup uniqueness. I’m now tracing whether restored rows are eligible before entering that snapshot, and whether reversal preserves the right values through table and column dormancy.
exec

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	//! Release-only storage outside the active row/index namespace.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#2-retain-by-default — moves, restorations and deletion evidence share activation's transaction.
     3	use super::*;
     4	use crate::schema::{ColumnDefault, ColumnType, materialize_optional_fields};
     5	use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
     6	use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
     7	
     8	pub(crate) const VERSION: &str = "snapback4-release-store-3";

    10	#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize)]
    11	struct Column {
    12	    kind: ColumnType,
    13	    default: Option<ColumnDefault>,
    14	    authoritative: bool,
    15	    #[serde(default)]

    17	}

    19	pub(crate) fn enable(conn: &Connection) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    20	    conn.execute_batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_narrow_rows(t INTEGER NOT NULL,id TEXT NOT NULL,data TEXT NOT NULL,definition TEXT NOT NULL,reason TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(t,id)) WITHOUT ROWID;
    21	        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_tables(table_name TEXT PRIMARY KEY, definition TEXT NOT NULL);
    22	        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_rows(t INTEGER NOT NULL,id TEXT NOT NULL,data TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(t,id)) WITHOUT ROWID;
    23	        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_columns(table_name TEXT NOT NULL,column_name TEXT NOT NULL,definition TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(table_name,column_name)) WITHOUT ROWID;
    24	        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_values(t INTEGER NOT NULL,id TEXT NOT NULL,column_name TEXT NOT NULL,data TEXT NOT NULL,live_source INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,PRIMARY KEY(t,id,column_name)) WITHOUT ROWID;
    25	        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_indexes(table_name TEXT NOT NULL,index_name TEXT NOT NULL,definition TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(table_name,index_name)) WITHOUT ROWID;
    26	        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_refs(t INTEGER NOT NULL,id TEXT NOT NULL,column_name TEXT NOT NULL,parent_t INTEGER NOT NULL,parent_id TEXT NOT NULL,invalid INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,PRIMARY KEY(t,id,column_name,parent_t,parent_id)) WITHOUT ROWID;
    27	        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_parent ON _dormant_refs(parent_t,parent_id);
    28	        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _release_events(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,at INTEGER NOT NULL,operation TEXT NOT NULL,release INTEGER,previous INTEGER,generation INTEGER NOT NULL,revision INTEGER NOT NULL,summary TEXT NOT NULL);
    29	        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _release_event_items(event INTEGER NOT NULL,path TEXT NOT NULL,detail TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(event,path)) WITHOUT ROWID;")?;
    30	    if !live_cells_column(conn)? {
    31	        // Published predecessors have captured-only cells. Defaulting them
    32	        // to captured is conservative; shared definitions cannot prove a
    33	        // particular retained cell ever had live authority.
    34	        conn.execute_batch("ALTER TABLE _dormant_values ADD COLUMN live_source INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;")?;
    35	    }
    36	    conn.execute("UPDATE _meta SET value=?1 WHERE key='version' AND value!='snapback4-release-store-4'", [VERSION])?;
    37	    Ok(())
    38	}
    39	
    40	pub(super) fn live_cells_column(conn:&Connection)->Result<bool,RuntimeError> {
    41	    Ok(conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM pragma_table_info('_dormant_values') WHERE name='live_source')",[],|r|r.get(0))?)
    42	}
    43	
    44	pub(super) fn enabled(tx: &Tx<'_>) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    45	    Ok(matches!(tx.meta("version")?.as_deref(),Some(VERSION|"snapback4-release-store-2"|super::backfills::VERSION)))
    46	}
    47	
    48	fn authoritative(table: &Table, column: &str) -> bool {
    49	    !table.maintained.contains_key(column) && !table.engine_field(column)
    50	}
    51	
    52	pub(super) fn definition(tx: &Tx<'_>, name: &str, table: &Table, column: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    53	    let value = Column {
    54	        kind: table.columns[column].clone(),
    55	        default: table.defaults.get(column).cloned(),
    56	        authoritative: authoritative(table, column),
    57	        capture_source: table.engine_field(column).then(||table.clone()),
    58	    };
    59	    tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _dormant_columns VALUES(?1,?2,?3) ON CONFLICT(table_name,column_name) DO UPDATE SET definition=excluded.definition", params![name,column,canonical_json(&value)])?;
    60	    Ok(())
    61	}
    62	
    63	fn references(kind: &ColumnType, value: &Value, out: &mut Vec<(String, String)>) {
    64	    match (kind, value) {
    65	        (ColumnType::Optional(inner), value) => references(inner, value, out),
    66	        (ColumnType::List { of, .. } | ColumnType::ListRange { of, .. }, Value::Array(values)) => {
    67	            for value in values {
    68	                references(of, value, out);
    69	            }
    70	        }
    71	        (ColumnType::Ref { table }, Value::Id(id)) => out.push((table.clone(), id.clone())),
    72	        _ => {}
    73	    }
    74	}
    75	
    76	fn rewrite_legacy_reference(
    77	    kind: &ColumnType,
    78	    value: &mut Value,
    79	    ids: &BTreeMap<String, BTreeMap<String, String>>,
    80	) -> bool {
    81	    match (kind, value) {
    82	        (ColumnType::Ref { table }, Value::Id(id)) => {
    83	            let Some(next) = ids.get(table).and_then(|ids| ids.get(id)) else { return false };
    84	            *id = next.clone();
    85	            true
    86	        }
    87	        (ColumnType::Optional(inner), value) => rewrite_legacy_reference(inner, value, ids),
    88	        (ColumnType::List { of, .. } | ColumnType::ListRange { of, .. }, Value::Array(values)) =>
    89	            values.iter_mut().fold(false, |changed, value| rewrite_legacy_reference(of, value, ids) || changed),
    90	        _ => false,
    91	    }
    92	}
    93	
    94	fn rewrite_legacy_row(
    95	    table: &Table,
    96	    row: &mut Value,
    97	    ids: &BTreeMap<String, BTreeMap<String, String>>,
    98	) -> bool {
    99	    let (Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields)) = row else { return false };
   100	    table.columns.iter().fold(false, |changed, (column, kind)| {
   101	        fields.get_mut(column).is_some_and(|value| rewrite_legacy_reference(kind, value, ids)) || changed
   102	    })
   103	}
   104	
   105	/// Rewrite references retained outside the active namespace alongside their
   106	/// active counterparts. References whose aggregate target was itself retired
   107	/// have no map and retain the ordinary dormant-lifetime refusal on restore.
   108	// @ref LLP 3000.007.003#3.0 — identity adoption covers every surviving typed reference, including dormant storage.
   109	pub(crate) fn rewrite_legacy_maintained_references(
   110	    tx: &Tx<'_>,
   111	    ids: &BTreeMap<String, BTreeMap<String, String>>,
   112	) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
   113	    if ids.values().all(BTreeMap::is_empty) || !enabled(tx)? { return Ok(0); }
   114	    let mut rewritten = 0_u64;
   115	
   116	    let cells = tx.conn().prepare(
   117	        "SELECT v.t,v.id,v.column_name,v.data,c.definition FROM _dormant_values v JOIN _catalog k ON k.num=v.t JOIN _dormant_columns c ON c.table_name=k.name AND c.column_name=v.column_name ORDER BY v.t,v.id,v.column_name"
   118	    )?.query_map([], |row| Ok((row.get::<_,i64>(0)?, row.get::<_,String>(1)?, row.get::<_,String>(2)?, row.get::<_,String>(3)?, row.get::<_,String>(4)?)))?
   119	        .collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?;
   120	    for (t, id, column, data, definition) in cells {
   121	        let definition: Column = serde_json::from_str(&definition).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
   122	        let mut value: Value = serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
   123	        if rewrite_legacy_reference(&definition.kind, &mut value, ids) {
   124	            tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _dormant_values SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3 AND column_name=?4", params![canonical_json(&value),t,id,column])?;
   125	            rewritten = rewritten.saturating_add(1);
   126	        }
   127	    }
   128	
   129	    let rows = tx.conn().prepare(
   130	        "SELECT r.t,r.id,r.data,d.definition FROM _dormant_rows r JOIN _catalog c ON c.num=r.t JOIN _dormant_tables d ON d.table_name=c.name ORDER BY r.t,r.id"
   131	    )?.query_map([], |row| Ok((row.get::<_,i64>(0)?, row.get::<_,String>(1)?, row.get::<_,String>(2)?, row.get::<_,String>(3)?)))?
   132	        .collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?;
   133	    for (t, id, data, definition) in rows {
   134	        let table: Table = serde_json::from_str(&definition).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
   135	        let mut row: Value = serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
   136	        if rewrite_legacy_row(&table, &mut row, ids) {
   137	            tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _dormant_rows SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3", params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;
   138	            rewritten = rewritten.saturating_add(1);
   139	        }
   140	    }
   141	
   142	    let narrowed = if super::narrowed::present(tx.conn())? {
   143	        tx.conn().prepare(
   144	            "SELECT t,id,data,definition FROM _dormant_narrow_rows ORDER BY t,id"
   145	        )?.query_map([], |row| Ok((row.get::<_,i64>(0)?, row.get::<_,String>(1)?, row.get::<_,String>(2)?, row.get::<_,String>(3)?)))?
   146	            .collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?
   147	    } else { Vec::new() };
   148	    for (t, id, data, definition) in narrowed {
   149	        let table: Table = serde_json::from_str(&definition).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
   150	        let mut row: Value = serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
   151	        if rewrite_legacy_row(&table, &mut row, ids) {
   152	            tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _dormant_narrow_rows SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3", params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;
   153	            rewritten = rewritten.saturating_add(1);
   154	        }
   155	    }
   156	
   157	    for (target, target_ids) in ids {
   158	        if target_ids.is_empty() { continue; }
   159	        let parent_t = tx.num(target)?;
   160	        for (old, next) in target_ids {
   161	            tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _dormant_refs(t,id,column_name,parent_t,parent_id,invalid) SELECT t,id,column_name,parent_t,?1,invalid FROM _dormant_refs WHERE parent_t=?2 AND parent_id=?3", params![next,parent_t,old])?;
   162	            tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_refs WHERE parent_t=?1 AND parent_id=?2", params![parent_t,old])?;
   163	        }
   164	    }
   165	    Ok(rewritten)
   166	}
   167	
   168	pub(super) fn capture_refs(
   169	    tx: &Tx<'_>,
   170	    t: i64,
   171	    id: &str,
   172	    column: &str,
   173	    kind: &ColumnType,
   174	    value: &Value,
   175	) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   176	    tx.conn().execute(
   177	        "DELETE FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",
   178	        params![t, id, column],
   179	    )?;
   180	    let mut refs = Vec::new();
   181	    references(kind, value, &mut refs);
   182	    for (parent, parent_id) in refs {
   183	        add_reference(tx, t, id, column, &parent, &parent_id)?;
   184	    }
   185	    Ok(())
   186	}
   187	
   188	fn add_reference(
   189	    tx: &Tx<'_>,
   190	    t: i64,
   191	    id: &str,
   192	    column: &str,
   193	    parent: &str,
   194	    parent_id: &str,
   195	) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   196	    let parent_t = tx.num(parent)?;
   197	    let invalid = !tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2)",params![parent_t,parent_id],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))?
   198	        && !tx.conn().query_row(
   199	            "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2)",
   200	            params![parent_t, parent_id],
   201	            |r| r.get::<_, bool>(0),
   202	        )?;
   203	    tx.conn().execute(
   204	        "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _dormant_refs VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5,?6)",
   205	        params![t, id, column, parent_t, parent_id, if invalid {2} else {0}],
   206	    )?;
   207	    Ok(())
   208	}
   209	pub(super) fn capture_through_parent(
   210	    tx: &Tx<'_>,
   211	    name: &str,
   212	    table: &Table,
   213	    id: &str,
   214	    column: &str,
   215	    row: &Value,
   216	) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   217	    if let Some(through) = &table.through {
   218	        if (through.fields.contains_key(column)
   219	            || matches!(
   220	                column,
   221	                crate::schema::through::PARENT_DEADLINE | crate::schema::through::DEADLINE
   222	            ))
   223	            && let Ok(Value::Id(parent_id)) = row.field(&through.reference)
   224	        {
   225	            add_reference(tx, tx.num(name)?, id, column, &through.parent, parent_id)?;
     1	//! Candidate activation: project rows, validate invariants, build indexes
     2	//! and derived rows, then publish the semantic generation in one commit.
     3	// @ref LLP 3000.000#implementation-follow-up — one atomic activation boundary
     4	
     5	pub(super) use super::activation_walk::walk;
     6	use super::activation_walk::walk_metered;
     7	use super::*;
     8	use crate::ir::Program;
     9	use crate::schema::{Constraint, materialize_optional_fields};
    10	use crate::value::digest;
    11	use std::collections::BTreeSet;

    13	pub(super) type Identity = (Option<String>, Option<String>, Option<String>, Option<String>,
    14	);
    15	
    16	pub(super) fn identity(conn: &Connection) -> Result<Identity, RuntimeError> {
    17	    let mut statement = conn.prepare_cached("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = ?1")?;
    18	    let mut get = |key| statement.query_row([key], |row| row.get(0)).optional();
    19	    Ok((get("schema_hash")?, get("activation_hash")?, get("generation")?, get("device:generation")?,
    20	    ))
    21	}
    22	
    23	impl Store {
    24	    /// Install a device's schema. Server engines also supply their programs
    25	    /// and derived-state builder through `activate` below.
    26	    pub fn install(&mut self, schema: &Schema) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    27	        self.activate(schema, None, false, |_, _| Ok(())).map(|_| ())
    28	    }
    29	
    30	    /// A device can discard an obsolete partition while atomically caching
    31	    /// its replacement backend and reset watermark. It never migrates rows
    32	    /// whose authority belongs to a previous server generation.
    33	    pub fn install_device(&mut self, schema: &Schema, metadata: &BTreeMap<String, String>, reset: bool) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    34	        self.install_device_checked(schema, metadata, reset, &BTreeMap::new())
    35	    }
    36	
    37	    pub fn install_device_checked(&mut self, schema: &Schema, metadata: &BTreeMap<String, String>, reset: bool, expected: &BTreeMap<String, Option<String>>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    38	        self.install_device_checked_with(schema, metadata, reset, expected, |_| Ok(()))
    39	    }
    40	
    41	    /// Device-owned sidecar changes share the schema installation transaction.
    42	    pub fn install_device_checked_with(&mut self, schema: &Schema, metadata: &BTreeMap<String, String>, reset: bool, expected: &BTreeMap<String, Option<String>>, prepare: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    43	        self.install_device_checked_with_finish(schema, metadata, reset, expected, prepare, |_| {
    44	            Ok(())
    45	        })
    46	    }
    47	
    48	    /// Opening recovery and its final reads must finish before installation commits.
    49	    pub fn install_device_checked_with_finish<R>(&mut self, schema: &Schema, metadata: &BTreeMap<String, String>, reset: bool, expected: &BTreeMap<String, Option<String>>, prepare: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>, finish: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>) -> Result<R, RuntimeError>,
    50	    ) -> Result<R, RuntimeError> {
    51	        let mut result = None;
    52	        self.activate(schema, None, reset, |tx, _| {
    53	            for (key, value) in expected {
    54	                let held: Option<String> = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = ?1", [key], |row| {
    55	                        row.get(0)
    56	                    }).optional()?;
    57	                if &held != value { return Err(RuntimeError::Generation("the backend adoption capture is obsolete; retry sync".into())); }
    58	            }
    59	            prepare(tx)?;
    60	            for (key, value) in metadata {
    61	                // A device metadata namespace reset shares backend installation's transaction.
    62	                if key.ends_with(':') {
    63	                    tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _meta WHERE key >= ?1 AND key < ?2", params![key, format!("{key}\u{10ffff}")])?;
    64	                } else { tx.set_meta(key, value)?; }
    65	            }
    66	            if reset { tx.conn().execute_batch("DELETE FROM _device_base; DELETE FROM _device_snapshot;")?; }
    67	            result = Some(finish(tx)?);
    68	            Ok(())
    69	        })?;
    70	        Ok(result.expect("activation ran opening recovery"))
    71	    }
    72	
    73	    pub(crate) fn activate(&mut self, schema: &Schema, programs: Option<&BTreeMap<String, Program>>, reset: bool, build: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>, bool) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
    74	        self.activate_at(schema, programs, reset, crate::now_ms(), |tx, changed, _| build(tx, changed))
    75	    }
    76	
    77	    pub(crate) fn activate_at(&mut self, schema: &Schema, programs: Option<&BTreeMap<String, Program>>, reset: bool, now: i64, build: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>, bool, &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
    78	        // One-shot device preparation is never retried or given a larger cache.
    79	        self.activate_transaction(schema, programs, reset, now, true, build)
    80	    }
    81	
    82	    pub(crate) fn activate_at_with_launch<R>(&mut self, schema: &Schema, programs: Option<&BTreeMap<String, Program>>, reset: bool, now: i64, allow_launch: bool, check: impl Fn(&Connection) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>, mut build: impl FnMut(&Tx<'_>, bool, &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>, finish: impl Fn(&Tx<'_>) -> Result<R, RuntimeError>,
    83	    ) -> Result<(u64, R), RuntimeError> {
    84	        let budget = if programs.is_some() { super::activation_cache::configured_mib()? } else { 0 };
    85	        // The server builder owns only transaction-local work. Recreate all of
    86	        // it on retry, with the same schema/programs/activation time.
    87	        self.with_activation_cache(budget, |store| {
    88	            store.activate_transaction_with(schema, programs, reset, now, allow_launch, &check, &mut build, &finish)
    89	        })
    90	    }
    91	
    92	    pub(super) fn activate_transaction(&mut self, schema: &Schema, programs: Option<&BTreeMap<String, Program>>, reset: bool, now: i64, allow_launch: bool, build: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>, bool, &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
    93	        self.activate_transaction_with(schema, programs, reset, now, allow_launch, |_| Ok(()), build, |_| Ok(())).map(|(generation, ())| generation)
    94	    }
    95	
    96	    fn activate_transaction_with<R>(&mut self, schema: &Schema, programs: Option<&BTreeMap<String, Program>>, reset: bool, now: i64, allow_launch: bool, check: impl FnOnce(&Connection) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>, build: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>, bool, &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>, finish: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>) -> Result<R, RuntimeError>,
    97	    ) -> Result<(u64, R), RuntimeError> {
    98	        super::live_activation::configure(&self.conn,schema)?;
    99	        let mut report = super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport::default();
   100	        let mut phase = std::time::Instant::now();
   101	        if programs.is_some() {
   102	            if let Some(estimate) = self.adoption_estimate(schema)?.filter(|e| e.seconds().1 > 1.0) {
   103	                crate::diagnostic!("snapback4: atomic adoption: {}", estimate.summary());
   104	            }
   105	            let estimates = self.check_timeline_activation_at(schema, now, allow_launch)?;
   106	            for estimate in estimates.iter().filter(|e| e.sources != 0) { crate::diagnostic!("snapback4: atomic feed adoption: {}", estimate.summary()); }
   107	        }
   108	        report.phase("read-only advice", &mut phase);
   109	        self.check_ownership()?;
   110	        let sql = self.conn.transaction_with_behavior(rusqlite::TransactionBehavior::Immediate)?;
   111	        // Low-level preparation may precede a writer-lock wait. Recheck all
   112	        // opening admission under the lock that owns the upgrades.
   113	        Self::check_version(&sql)?;
   114	        let before = identity(&sql)?;
   115	        if before != self.identity { return Err(RuntimeError::Generation("another engine activated this store; reopen it before activating source".into())); }
   116	        check(&sql)?;
   117	        // @ref LLP 3000.033#1.3 — physical upgrades roll back with adoption.
   118	        // Rollback restores schema/rows and freelist count. It may leave
   119	        // changed free-page contents or journal/WAL residue, not committed data.
   120	        Self::initialize_schema(&sql)?;
   121	        if programs.is_some() { Self::initialize_change_evidence(&sql)?; }
   122	        index_key_guards(&sql)?;
   123	        // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.6 — reuse an admitted key bound, never assume a legacy store was checked.
   124	        // Physical INSERT/UPDATE triggers invalidate the proof on oversized keys,
   125	        // including direct SQL. Every activation key
   126	        // below is checked too. Bind the proof to the predecessor so an older
   127	        // binary's adoption invalidates it; device installation always scans.
   128	        let key_proof = before.1.as_ref().map(|hash| format!("{}:{hash}", crate::rows::MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES));
   129	        let validated: Option<String> = sql.query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = 'index_keys_validated'", [], |r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   130	        let keys_validated = programs.is_some() && key_proof.is_some() && validated == key_proof;
   131	        #[cfg(test)]
   132	        let keys_validated = keys_validated && !self.full_adoption;
   133	        // One bounded result, no row images. Fresh stores have no schema;
   134	        // an explicit device reset discards the old entries in this transaction.
   135	        if before.0.is_some() && !reset && !keys_validated {
   136	            let violation: Option<(String, i64)> = sql.query_row(
   137	                "SELECT c.name, COUNT(*) FROM i JOIN _catalog c ON c.num = i.ti WHERE length(i.k) > ?1 GROUP BY i.ti ORDER BY i.ti LIMIT 1",
   138	                [crate::rows::MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES as i64], |row| Ok((row.get(0)?, row.get(1)?))
   139	            ).optional()?;
   140	            if let Some((name, count)) = violation {
   141	                let (table, index) = name.rsplit_once('.').unwrap_or((&name, "unknown"));
   142	                return Err(crate::rows::index_key_refusal(table, index, count as u64));
   143	            }
   144	        }
   145	        report.phase("key validation", &mut phase);
   146	        let schema_hash = schema.hash();
   147	        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#6-devices-and-partitions — executable native content changes the existing semantic generation.
   148	        let native: Option<String> = sql.query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key='release_native_digest'", [], |r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   149	        let activation_hash = programs.map(|programs| match &native {Some(native)=>digest(&(schema,programs,native)),None=>digest(&(schema,programs))});
   150	        let schema_changed = before.0.as_deref() != Some(&schema_hash);
   151	        let mut changed = schema_changed || activation_hash.as_ref().is_some_and(|hash| before.1.as_ref() != Some(hash));
   152	        // The address function is part of the semantic generation even when
   153	        // the authored schema and programs are byte-identical. Devices reset
   154	        // with that generation; the server rebuilds old target rows below.
   155	        let predecessor_has_aggregate = sql.query_row(
   156	            "SELECT schema FROM _generations WHERE generation = (SELECT CAST(value AS INTEGER) FROM _meta WHERE key = 'generation')",
   157	            [], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0),
   158	        ).optional()?.map(|text| serde_json::from_str::<Schema>(&text)
   159	            .map_err(|error| RuntimeError::decode(error, RuntimeError::Store))).transpose()?
   160	            .is_some_and(|previous| previous.maintains.iter().any(|maintain| maintain.row_key.is_none()));
   161	        let maintained_identity_upgrade = programs.is_some()
   162	            && predecessor_has_aggregate
   163	            && sql.query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key=?1", [crate::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_META], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0)).optional()?.as_deref()
   164	                != Some(crate::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_VERSION);
   165	        changed |= maintained_identity_upgrade;
   166	        let mut catalog: BTreeMap<String, i64> = sql.prepare("SELECT name, num FROM _catalog")?
   167	            .query_map([], |row| Ok((row.get(0)?, row.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
   168	        // Repeat source-count admission under the writer lock, before any candidate writes.
   169	        let preflight_catalog = catalog.clone();
   170	        let (launches, sql) = {
   171	            let preflight = Tx { activation_scan: None, changes: Default::default(), sequence_table: Default::default(), tx: sql, page_size: self.page_size,
   172	                server: self.server,
   173	                catalog: &preflight_catalog };
   174	            let launches = if programs.is_some() { super::timeline_launch::preflight(&preflight, schema, now, allow_launch)? } else { Vec::new() };
   175	            let Tx { tx: sql, .. } = preflight;
   176	            (launches, sql)
   177	        };
   178	        let mut next = catalog.values().copied().max().unwrap_or(0) + 1;
   179	        for (name, table) in schema.tables.iter().filter(|(_,t)| t.ephemeral.is_none()) {
   180	            for full in std::iter::once(name.clone()).chain(table.indexes.keys().map(|index| format!("{name}.{index}"))) {
   181	                if !catalog.contains_key(&full) {
   182	                    sql.execute("INSERT INTO _catalog(name, num) VALUES (?1, ?2)", params![full, next])?;
   183	                    catalog.insert(full, next); next += 1;
   184	                }
   185	            }
   186	        }
   187	        let activation_scan = programs.map(|_| Default::default());
   188	        #[cfg(test)]
   189	        let activation_scan = if self.full_adoption { None } else { activation_scan };
   190	        let tx = Tx { activation_scan, changes: Default::default(), sequence_table: Default::default(), tx: sql, page_size: self.page_size,
   191	            server: self.server,
   192	            catalog: &catalog };
   193	        let now = if schema.tables.values().any(|table| table.not_before.is_some()) {
   194	            tx.pin_time_audience_clock(now)?
   195	        } else {
   196	            now
   197	        };
   198	        // @ref LLP 3000.013#12.1 — occupancy belongs to successful activation,
   199	        // never opening a legacy store that may subsequently refuse adoption.
   200	        let cap_counts = crate::rows::caps::required(schema) || tx.meta("cap_counts_schema")?.is_some();
   201	        if cap_counts {
   202	            tx.conn().execute_batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _cap_counts (t INTEGER NOT NULL, k BLOB NOT NULL, n INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(n >= 0), PRIMARY KEY(t, k)) WITHOUT ROWID;")?;
   203	            if reset { tx.conn().execute_batch("DELETE FROM _cap_counts;")?; }
   204	        }
   205	        if schema.tables.values().any(|t| !t.sequences.is_empty()) {
   206	            tx.ensure_sequence_table()?;
   207	        }
   208	        if reset {
   209	            tx.conn().execute_batch("DELETE FROM r; DELETE FROM i;")?;
   210	            if tx.has_sequence_table()? { tx.conn().execute_batch("DELETE FROM _sequences;")?; }
   211	        }
   212	        super::live_activation::clear_stale(&tx)?;
   213	        let mut retained_through=BTreeSet::new();
   214	        let release_activation=programs.is_some() && tx.meta("activation_restore")?.is_some();
   215	        if (schema_changed || release_activation) && programs.is_some() && !reset {
   216	            let previous: Option<String> = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT schema FROM _generations WHERE generation=(SELECT CAST(value AS INTEGER) FROM _meta WHERE key='generation')", [], |r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   217	            let previous: Option<Schema> = previous.map(|s|serde_json::from_str(&s).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))).transpose()?;
   218	            let restore=tx.meta("activation_restore")?.as_deref()!=Some("false");
   219	            retained_through=super::dormant::transition(&tx,schema,previous.as_ref(),restore,now,&mut report)?;
   220	        }
   221	        let population_changed=tx.meta("activation_population")?.as_deref()==Some("true");
   222	        changed |= population_changed;
   223	        let rebuilding=schema_changed || population_changed;
   224	        if rebuilding && programs.is_some() && !reset {
   225	            let started = std::time::Instant::now();
   226	            let previous: Option<String> = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT schema FROM _generations WHERE generation = (SELECT CAST(value AS INTEGER) FROM _meta WHERE key = 'generation')", [], |row| row.get(0)).optional()?;
   227	            let previous: Option<Schema> = previous.map(|s| {
   228	                    serde_json::from_str(&s).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::decode(e,RuntimeError::Store))
   229	                }).transpose()?;
   230	            let shortened = crate::engine::through_activation::activate_retained(&tx,schema,previous.as_ref(),now,&retained_through)?;
   231	            tx.set_meta("read_through_shortened",&canonical_json(&shortened))?;
   232	            report.detail("read-through retirement", started);
   233	        }
   234	        if programs.is_some() { crate::engine::timeline::check_activation(&tx, schema)?; }
   235	        if rebuilding && programs.is_some() {
   236	            let started = std::time::Instant::now();
   237	            super::timeline_launch::build(&tx, schema, &launches, now)?;
   238	            report.detail("feed launch", started);
   239	            let started = std::time::Instant::now();
   240	            super::event_slots::fill(&tx, schema, now)?;
   241	            report.detail("event slot fill", started);
   242	            let started = std::time::Instant::now();
   243	            if super::dormant::enabled(&tx)? {
   244	                let old:Option<String>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT schema FROM _generations WHERE generation=(SELECT CAST(value AS INTEGER) FROM _meta WHERE key='generation')",[],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   245	                let old:Option<Schema>=old.map(|s| {
   246	                        serde_json::from_str(&s).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))
   247	                    }).transpose()?;
   248	                super::adopt_refusals::candidate(&tx,schema,if tx.meta("activation_population")?.as_deref()==Some("true"){None}else{old.as_ref()},false)?;
   249	            }
   250	            if let Some(step) = super::successor::assess(&tx, schema)?.iter().find(|s| s.change == crate::successor::Change::Narrowing) {
   251	                return Err(step.refusal(schema));
   252	            }
   253	            report.detail("successor assessment", started);
   254	        }
   255	        let previous: Option<Schema> = if rebuilding && programs.is_some() && !reset {
   256	            tx.conn().query_row("SELECT schema FROM _generations WHERE generation = (SELECT CAST(value AS INTEGER) FROM _meta WHERE key = 'generation')", [], |r| r.get::<_, String>(0)).optional()?
   257	                .map(|s| serde_json::from_str(&s).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::decode(e, RuntimeError::Store))).transpose()?
   258	        } else { None };
   259	        #[cfg(test)]
   260	        let previous = if self.full_adoption { None } else { previous };

   230	
   231	pub(super) fn save_cell(
   232	    tx: &Tx<'_>,
   233	    name: &str,
   234	    table: &Table,
   235	    id: &str,
   236	    column: &str,
   237	    value: &Value,
   238	    capture: bool,
   239	) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   240	    let t = tx.num(name)?;
   241	    // @ref LLP 3000.014.000#implementation-proof-clarifications — another row cannot overwrite a cell's live origin.
   242	    let live=table.engine_field(column) && table.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|e|e.live.is_some());
   243	    tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _dormant_values(t,id,column_name,data,live_source) VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5) ON CONFLICT(t,id,column_name) DO UPDATE SET data=excluded.data,live_source=excluded.live_source",params![t,id,column,canonical_json(value),i64::from(live)])?;
   244	    if capture && !super::authority_origin::live_input(table,column) {
   245	        capture_refs(tx, t, id, column, &table.columns[column], value)
   246	    } else {
   247	        Ok(())
   248	    }
   249	}

   251	fn refuse(message: String) -> RuntimeError {
   252	    RuntimeError::Structural {
   253	        code: "E_ADOPT",
   254	        message,
   255	    }
   256	}

   258	pub(super) fn check_owner(tx: &Tx<'_>, name: &str, old: &Table, next: &Schema) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   259	    let Some(new) = next.table(name) else {
   260	        return Ok(());
   261	    };
   262	    let evidence = || -> Result<String, RuntimeError> {
   263	        let mut samples = super::adopt_refusals::Samples::default();
   264	        super::activation::walk(tx, tx.num(name)?, |id, _| {
   265	            samples.add(id, "storage ownership");
   266	            Ok(())
   267	        })?;
   268	        if samples.count == 0 {
   269	            let t = tx.num(name)?;
   270	            let count: i64 =
   271	                tx.conn()
   272	                    .query_row("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1", [t], |r| {
   273	                        r.get(0)
   274	                    })?;
   275	            let ids = tx
   276	                .conn()
   277	                .prepare("SELECT id FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 ORDER BY id LIMIT 5")?
   278	                .query_map([t], |r| r.get::<_, String>(0))?
   279	                .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;
   280	            return Ok(format!(
   281	                "{count} affected rows; sample ids [{}]",
   282	                ids.iter()
   283	                    .map(|i| super::adopt_refusals::bounded(i))
   284	                    .collect::<Vec<_>>()
   285	                    .join(", ")
   286	            ));
   287	        }
   288	        Ok(samples.describe())
   289	    };
   290	    if new.ephemeral.is_some() {
   291	        return Err(refuse(format!(
   292	            "table {name}: {}; durable storage cannot become ephemeral; deploy without {name}, run `snapback4 prune <dir> --allow-destructive`, then deploy the ephemeral table",
   293	            evidence()?
   294	        )));
   295	    }
   296	    if next.maintain_for(name).is_some() {
   297	        return Err(refuse(format!(
   298	            "table {name}: {}; authoritative storage cannot become a derived table; rename the derived table, or retire {name}, run `snapback4 prune <dir> --allow-destructive`, then deploy",
   299	            evidence()?
   300	        )));
   301	    }
   302	    for column in old
   303	        .columns
   304	        .keys()
   305	        .filter(|column| authoritative(old, column))
   306	    {
   307	        if new.columns.contains_key(column) && !authoritative(new, column) {
   308	            return Err(refuse(format!(
   309	                "column {name}.{column}: {}; authoritative values cannot become maintained/captured values; rename the new column, or retire it, run `snapback4 prune <dir> --allow-destructive`, then deploy",
   310	                evidence()?
   311	            )));
   312	        }
   313	    }
   314	    Ok(())
   315	}
   316	
   317	/// Restore all parents before checking any retained child's lifetime. Consume
   318	/// only candidate-visible cells, so partial table restoration is reversible.
   319	pub(super) fn transition(
   320	    tx: &Tx<'_>,
   321	    next: &Schema,
   322	    previous: Option<&Schema>,
   323	    restore: bool,
   324	    now:i64,
   325	    report: &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport,
   326	) -> Result<BTreeSet<String>, RuntimeError> {
   327	    if !enabled(tx)? {
   328	        return Ok(BTreeSet::new());
   329	    }
   330	    if !super::narrowed::present(tx.conn())? || !live_cells_column(tx.conn())? {enable(tx.conn())?;}
   331	    let whole: Vec<(String, String)> = tx
   332	        .conn()
   333	        .prepare("SELECT table_name,definition FROM _dormant_tables ORDER BY table_name")?
   334	        .query_map([], |r| Ok((r.get(0)?, r.get(1)?)))?
   335	        .collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
   336	    let mut restoring = Vec::new();
   337	    let mut retained_through = BTreeSet::new();
   338	    for (name, text) in &whole {
   339	        let old: Table =
   340	            serde_json::from_str(text).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
   341	        check_owner(tx, name, &old, next)?;
   342	        if next.tables.contains_key(name) {
   343	            if old.through.is_some() {
   344	                retained_through.insert(name.clone());
   345	            }
   346	            restoring.push((name.clone(), old));
   347	        }
   348	    }
   349	    let cells:Vec<(String,String,String)>=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT table_name,column_name,definition FROM _dormant_columns ORDER BY table_name,column_name")?.query_map([],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?,r.get(2)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
   350	    let mut reuse: Vec<String> = restoring.iter().map(|(n, _)| n.clone()).collect();
   351	    for (name, column, text) in &cells {
   352	        if let Some(table) = next.table(name).filter(|t| t.columns.contains_key(column)) {
   353	            if column == crate::schema::through::PARENT_DEADLINE {
   354	                retained_through.insert(name.clone());
   355	            }
   356	            let old: Column =
   357	                serde_json::from_str(text).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
   358	            if let Some(source)=&old.capture_source {
   359	                if source.through.is_some() && source.through == table.through {retained_through.insert(name.clone());}
   360	                else if source.through.as_ref().zip(table.through.as_ref()).is_some_and(|(a,b)| (a.live.is_some() || b.live.is_some()) && a.reference==b.reference && a.parent==b.parent) { /* Restamp destination; live provenance never retains historical authority. */ }
   361	                else if source.through.is_some() && table.through.is_some() {return Err(refuse(format!("{name}: retained engine captures belong to another read-through definition; restore the original definition before changing the audience")));}
   362	            }
   363	            if old.authoritative && !authoritative(table, column) {
   364	                let mut samples = super::adopt_refusals::Samples::default();
   365	                let t = tx.num(name)?;
   366	                let mut statement = tx.conn().prepare(
   367	                    "SELECT id,data FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2 ORDER BY id",
   368	                )?;
   369	                for row in statement.query_map(params![t, column], |r| {
   370	                    Ok((r.get::<_, String>(0)?, r.get::<_, String>(1)?))
   371	                })? {
   372	                    let (id, value) = row?;
   373	                    samples.add(&id, &super::adopt_refusals::bounded(&value));
   374	                }
   375	                return Err(refuse(format!(
   376	                    "column {name}.{column}: {}; dormant authoritative values cannot become maintained/captured; rename the new column, or run `snapback4 prune <dir> --allow-destructive` while it is dormant, then deploy",
   377	                    samples.describe()
   378	                )));
   379	            }
   380	            if previous.and_then(|s|s.table(name)).is_none_or(|t|!t.columns.contains_key(column)) {reuse.push(format!("{name}.{column}"));}
   381	        }
   382	    }
   383	    let indexes: Vec<(String, String)> = tx
   384	        .conn()
   385	        .prepare(
   386	            "SELECT table_name,index_name FROM _dormant_indexes ORDER BY table_name,index_name",
   387	        )?
   388	        .query_map([], |r| Ok((r.get(0)?, r.get(1)?)))?
   389	        .collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
   390	    for (name, index) in &indexes {
   391	        if next
   392	            .table(name)
   393	            .is_some_and(|t| t.indexes.contains_key(index))
   394	        {
   395	            reuse.push(format!("{name}.{index}"));
   396	        }
   397	    }
   398	    if !restore && !reuse.is_empty() {
   399	        let inventory = super::release_history::inventory(tx.conn(), None, usize::MAX)?;
   400	        return Err(refuse(format!(
   401	            "dormant names [{}] require restoration intent; retained storage: {}; proceed with `snapback4 deploy <dir> --restore-dormant`, or `snapback4 prune <dir> --allow-destructive` before fresh creation",
   402	            reuse.join(", "),
   403	            inventory.iter().map(|i|format!("{}: {} rows; sample ids [{}]",i.key,i.rows,i.sample_ids.join(", "))).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("; ")
   404	        )));
   405	    }
   406	    if let Some(previous) = previous {
   407	        for (name, old) in previous
   408	            .tables
   409	            .iter()
   410	            .filter(|(n, t)| t.ephemeral.is_none() && previous.maintain_for(n).is_none())
   411	        {
   412	            check_owner(tx, name, old, next)?;
   413	            let candidate = next.table(name);
   414	            let removed: Vec<_> = old
   415	                .columns
   416	                .keys()
   417	                .filter(|c| candidate.is_none_or(|t| !t.columns.contains_key(*c)))
   418	                .cloned()
   419	                .collect();
   420	            for column in &removed {
   421	                definition(tx, name, old, column)?;
   422	            }
   423	            for (index, definition) in &old.indexes {
   424	                if candidate.is_none_or(|t| !t.indexes.contains_key(index)) {
   425	                    tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _dormant_indexes VALUES(?1,?2,?3) ON CONFLICT(table_name,index_name) DO UPDATE SET definition=excluded.definition",params![name,index,canonical_json(definition)])?;
   426	                }
   427	            }
   428	            if candidate.is_none() {
   429	                tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _dormant_tables VALUES(?1,?2) ON CONFLICT(table_name) DO UPDATE SET definition=excluded.definition",params![name,canonical_json(old)])?;
   430	            }
   431	            if removed.is_empty() && candidate.is_some() {
   432	                continue;
   433	            }
   434	            super::activation::walk(tx, tx.num(name)?, |id, mut row| {
   435	                materialize_optional_fields(&mut row, old);
   436	                if candidate.is_none() {
   437	                    tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _dormant_rows VALUES(?1,?2,?3) ON CONFLICT(t,id) DO UPDATE SET data=excluded.data",params![tx.num(name)?,id,canonical_json(&row)])?;
   438	                    for (column, kind) in &old.columns {
   439	                        if let Ok(value) = row.field(column) {
   440	                            if !super::authority_origin::live_input(old,column) {capture_refs(tx, tx.num(name)?, id, column, kind, value)?;}
   441	                            capture_through_parent(tx, name, old, id, column, &row)?;
   442	                        }
   443	                    }
   444	                } else {
   445	                    for column in &removed {
   446	                        if let Ok(value) = row.field(column) {
   447	                            save_cell(tx, name, old, id, column, value, true)?;
   448	                            capture_through_parent(tx, name, old, id, column, &row)?;
   449	                        }
   450	                    }
   451	                }
   452	                Ok(())
   453	            })?;
   454	            if candidate.is_none() {
   455	                tx.conn()
   456	                    .execute("DELETE FROM r WHERE t=?1", [tx.num(name)?])?;
   457	                for index in old.indexes.keys() {
   458	                    tx.conn().execute(
   459	                        "DELETE FROM i WHERE ti=?1",
   460	                        [tx.num(&format!("{name}.{index}"))?],
   461	                    )?;
   462	                }
   463	            }
   464	        }
   465	    }
   466	    for (name, old) in &restoring {
   467	        let t = tx.num(name)?;
   468	        let table = &next.tables[name];
   469	        tx.conn().execute(
   470	            "INSERT INTO r(t,id,data) SELECT t,id,data FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1",
   471	            [t],
   472	        )?;
   473	        for column in old
   474	            .columns
   475	            .keys()
   476	            .filter(|c| !table.columns.contains_key(*c))
   477	        {
   478	            definition(tx, name, old, column)?;
   479	        }
   480	        super::activation::walk(tx, t, |id, row| {
   481	            let removed: Vec<_> = row
   482	                .as_object()?
   483	                .iter()
   484	                .filter(|(c, _)| !table.columns.contains_key(*c))
   485	                .map(|(c, v)| (c.clone(), v.clone()))
   486	                .collect();
   487	            for (column, value) in removed {
   488	                if old.columns.contains_key(&column) {
   489	                    save_cell(tx, name, old, id, &column, &value, false)?;
   490	                }
   491	            }
   492	            // Full source image/deadlines stay intact until lifetime retirement and narrowed classification.
   493	            Ok(())
   494	        })?;
   495	        if super::live_activation::active(tx)? {
   496	            tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._live_restored SELECT 'table',t,'',id FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1",[t])?;
   497	            if old.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|e|e.live.is_some()) {tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._live_restored SELECT 'live-authority',t,'',id FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1",[t])?;}
   498	        } else {
   499	            let ids=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT id FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1")?.query_map([t],|r|r.get::<_,String>(0))?.collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?;
   500	            tx.set_meta(&format!("activation_table_restored_ids:{name}"),&serde_json::to_string(&ids).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?)?;
   501	        }
   502	        tx.conn()
   503	            .execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1", [t])?;
   504	        tx.conn()
   505	            .execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_tables WHERE table_name=?1", [name])?;
   506	    }
   507	    let mut authority_sources=previous.map(|s|s.tables.clone()).unwrap_or_default();
   508	    for (name,table) in &restoring {authority_sources.insert(name.clone(),table.clone());}
   509	    crate::engine::through_activation::reconcile_manual(tx,next,&authority_sources,now)?;
   510	    for (name, table) in next.tables.iter().filter(|(_, t)| t.ephemeral.is_none()) {
   511	        let t = tx.num(name)?;
   512	        if !cells
   513	            .iter()
   514	            .any(|(n, c, _)| n == name && table.columns.contains_key(c))
   515	            && !restoring.iter().any(|(n, _)| n == name)
   516	        {
   517	            continue;
   518	        }
   519	        let old=authority_sources.get(name);
   520	        let parent_scratch=if super::live_activation::active(tx)? {

exec
