 succeeded in 0ms:
   520	        let parent_scratch=if super::live_activation::active(tx)? {
   521	            old.map_or(0,|old|crate::engine::through_activation::restored_parent_scratch(next,&authority_sources,name,old))
   522	        } else {0};
   523	        let scratch=|row:&Value|->Result<crate::value::InternalPrice,RuntimeError> {
   524	            let fetch=super::authority_origin::forward_fetch(tx,t,row.field("id")?.as_str()?,old,table)?;
   525	            Ok(crate::value::InternalPrice::zero().fixed(if fetch {parent_scratch} else {0}))
   526	        };
   527	        super::activation_walk::walk_metered_checked(tx,t,crate::rows::adoption::Work::DECODE,if parent_scratch>0 {Some(&scratch)} else {None},|id,mut row,meter| {
   528	            let old=authority_sources.get(name);
   529	            if let Some(old)=old {
   530	                materialize_optional_fields(&mut row,old);
   531	                super::authority_origin::validate(tx,next,&authority_sources,name,old,&row,now,false)?;
   532	                if restoring.iter().any(|(n,_)|n==name) {super::authority_origin::record(tx,t,id,old)?;}
   533	            }
   534	            let values=restored_values(tx,t,id,table,meter)?;
   535	            super::authority_origin::overlay(tx,t,id,old,table,&mut row,values)?;
   536	            tx.conn().execute(
   537	                "UPDATE r SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3",
   538	                params![canonical_json(&row), t, id],
   539	            )?;
   540	            Ok(())
   541	        })?;
   542	    }
   543	    for name in next.tables.keys() {
   544	        if next.tables[name].through.is_some() && tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1)",[tx.num(name)?],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))? {retained_through.insert(name.clone());}
   545	    }
   546	    let mut source_definitions=previous.map(|s|s.tables.clone()).unwrap_or_default();
   547	    for (name,table) in &restoring {source_definitions.insert(name.clone(),table.clone());}
   548	    crate::engine::through_activation::forward_sources(tx,next,&authority_sources,now)?;
   549	    for (name,column,text) in &cells {
   550	        if let Some(candidate)=next.table(name).filter(|t|t.columns.contains_key(column)) {
   551	            let old:Column=serde_json::from_str(text).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
   552	            let source=source_definitions.entry(name.clone()).or_insert_with(||candidate.clone());
   553	            if let Some(capture)=&old.capture_source {
   554	                source.through=capture.through.clone();source.expire=capture.expire.clone();
   555	
   556	            }
   557	            if super::live_activation::active(tx)? && authority_sources.get(name).is_none_or(|t|t.through.as_ref().is_none_or(|e|e.live.is_some())) {
   558	                // Cell provenance survives shared definition changes. Immediate
   559	                // manual correspondence above still precedes this marker.
   560	                tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._live_restored SELECT 'live-authority',v.t,'',v.id FROM _dormant_values v WHERE v.t=?1 AND v.column_name=?2 AND v.live_source=1 AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=v.t AND r.id=v.id) AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE n.t=v.t AND n.id=v.id)",params![tx.num(name)?,column])?;
   561	            }
   562	            source.columns.insert(column.clone(),old.kind);
   563	            if let Some(default)=old.default {source.defaults.insert(column.clone(),default);}
   564	            if !old.authoritative {
   565	                if let Some(owner)=candidate.maintained.get(column) {source.maintained.insert(column.clone(),owner.clone());}
   566	                if let Some(capture)=candidate.recorded.get(column) {source.recorded.insert(column.clone(),capture.clone());}
   567	            }
   568	        }
   569	    }
   570	    let restored_images=!restoring.is_empty() || cells.iter().any(|(n,c,_)|next.table(n).is_some_and(|t|t.columns.contains_key(c)));
   571	    // Current authority cells precede destination narrowing: historical
   572	    // authored copies may be outside a newly narrowed type even though the
   573	    // current parent value fits it. Newly restored narrowed rows reconcile
   574	    // individually inside transition as well.
   575	    crate::engine::through_activation::reconcile_manual(tx,next,&authority_sources,now)?;
   576	    super::narrowed::retire_sources(tx,next,previous,now)?;
   577	    if !next.backfills.is_empty() || super::backfills::has_evidence(tx)? {
   578	        super::narrowed::restore_images(tx,next,now,&source_definitions,&authority_sources,true)?;
   579	    }
   580	    super::backfills::transition(tx,next,previous,&source_definitions,now,report)?;
   581	    super::narrowed::transition(tx,next,previous,now,restored_images,&source_definitions,&authority_sources)?;
   582	    crate::engine::through_activation::reconcile_manual(tx,next,&authority_sources,now)?;
   583	    check_references(tx, next,&authority_sources)?;
   584	    // Restored rows have no predecessor indexes. Install the identity scan
   585	    // before the ordinary read-through barrier; preserve historical captures.
   586	    let identity_tables:BTreeSet<String>=if tx.meta("activation_population")?.as_deref()==Some("true") {
   587	        next.tables.iter().filter(|(n,t)|t.ephemeral.is_none() && next.maintain_for(n).is_none()).map(|(n,_)|n.clone()).collect()
   588	    } else {restoring.iter().map(|(n,_)|n.clone()).collect()};
   589	    for name in &identity_tables {
   590	        let table = &next.tables[name];
   591	        let t = tx.num(name)?;
   592	        let index = tx.num(&format!("{name}.byId"))?;
   593	        tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM i WHERE ti=?1",[index])?;
   594	        super::activation::walk(tx, t, |id, row| {
   595	            for key in index_keys(&table.indexes["byId"], &row, id) {
   596	                tx.conn().execute(
   597	                    "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO i(ti,k,id) VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",
   598	                    params![index, key, id],
   599	                )?;
   600	            }
   601	            Ok(())
   602	        })?;
   603	    }
   604	    for (name, table) in next.tables.iter().filter(|(_, t)| t.ephemeral.is_none()) {
   605	        let t = tx.num(name)?;
   606	        for column in table.columns.keys() {
   607	            if super::live_activation::active(tx)? {
   608	                tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._live_restored SELECT 'column',v.t,v.column_name,v.id FROM _dormant_values v WHERE v.t=?1 AND v.column_name=?2 AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=v.t AND r.id=v.id) AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE n.t=v.t AND n.id=v.id)",params![t,column])?;
   609	            } else {
   610	                let ids=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT v.id FROM _dormant_values v WHERE v.t=?1 AND v.column_name=?2 AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=v.t AND r.id=v.id) AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE n.t=v.t AND n.id=v.id)")?.query_map(params![t,column],|r|r.get::<_,String>(0))?.collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?;
   611	                if !ids.is_empty() {tx.set_meta(&format!("activation_column_restored_ids:{name}.{column}"),&serde_json::to_string(&ids).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?)?;}
   612	            }
   613	            tx.conn().execute(
   614	                "DELETE FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2 AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE n.t=_dormant_values.t AND n.id=_dormant_values.id)",
   615	                params![t, column],
   616	            )?;
   617	            tx.conn().execute(
   618	                "DELETE FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2 AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE n.t=_dormant_refs.t AND n.id=_dormant_refs.id)",
   619	                params![t, column],
   620	            )?;
   621	            tx.conn().execute(
   622	                "DELETE FROM _dormant_columns WHERE table_name=?1 AND column_name=?2 AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?3 AND column_name=?2)",
   623	                params![name, column,t],
   624	            )?;
   625	        }
   626	        for index in table.indexes.keys() {
   627	            tx.conn().execute(
   628	                "DELETE FROM _dormant_indexes WHERE table_name=?1 AND index_name=?2",
   629	                params![name, index],
   630	            )?;
   631	        }
   632	    }
   633	    Ok(retained_through)
   634	}
   635	
   636	fn check_references(tx: &Tx<'_>, next: &Schema,sources:&BTreeMap<String,Table>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   637	    for (name, table) in next.tables.iter().filter(|(_, t)| t.ephemeral.is_none()) {
   638	        let t = tx.num(name)?;
   639	        let mut bad = super::adopt_refusals::Samples::default();
   640	        let mut statement=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT DISTINCT d.id,d.column_name,d.parent_id,d.invalid FROM _dormant_refs d LEFT JOIN r p ON p.t=d.parent_t AND p.id=d.parent_id WHERE d.t=?1 AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r child WHERE child.t=d.t AND child.id=d.id) AND (d.invalid=1 OR (p.id IS NULL AND d.invalid!=2)) ORDER BY d.id,d.column_name")?;
   641	        let mut rows = statement.query([t])?;
   642	        while let Some(row) = rows.next()? {
   643	            let column: String = row.get(1)?;
   644	            let id:String=row.get(0)?;
   645	            if table.columns.contains_key(&column) && !super::authority_origin::obsolete_ref(tx,t,&id,&column,table,sources.get(name))? {
   646	                bad.add(
   647	                    &row.get::<_, String>(0)?,
   648	                    &format!(
   649	                        "{column} → parent {} (deleted/reused/missing)",
   650	                        row.get::<_, String>(2)?
   651	                    ),
   652	                );
   653	            }
   654	        }
   655	        if bad.count > 0 {
   656	            return Err(refuse(format!(
   657	                "table {name}: {}; dormant references cannot acquire another parent lifetime; keep the current release and repair the named references, or prune the dormant storage before restoring",
   658	                bad.describe()
   659	            )));
   660	        }
   661	        if let Some(through) = &table.through {
   662	            {
   663	                let mut bad = super::adopt_refusals::Samples::default();
   664	                super::activation_walk::walk_metered(tx,t,crate::rows::adoption::Work::DECODE,None,|id,row,meter| {
   665	                    let restored: bool = tx.conn().query_row(
   666	                        "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2)",
   667	                        params![t, id],
   668	                        |r| r.get(0),
   669	                    )?;
   670	                    if !restored {
   671	                        return Ok(());
   672	                    }
   673	                    // The captured deadline belongs to one parent lifetime,
   674	                    // even if an older schema allowed retargeting the active ref.
   675	                    let parent_t = tx.num(&through.parent)?;
   676	                    let current = row
   677	                        .field(&through.reference)
   678	                        .ok()
   679	                        .and_then(|v| v.as_str().ok());
   680	                    let mut evidence=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT parent_t,parent_id,(SELECT name FROM _catalog WHERE num=parent_t) FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3")?;
   681	                    for edge in evidence.query_map(
   682	                        params![t, id, crate::schema::through::PARENT_DEADLINE],
   683	                        |r| {
   684	                            Ok((
   685	                                r.get::<_, i64>(0)?,
   686	                                r.get::<_, String>(1)?,
   687	                                r.get::<_, String>(2)?,
   688	                            ))
   689	                        },
   690	                    )? {
   691	                        let (old_table, old_id, old_name) = edge?;
   692	                        if old_table != parent_t || current != Some(old_id.as_str()) {
   693	                            bad.add(
   694	                                id,
   695	                                &format!(
   696	                                    "{}.{}: recorded {} [{}], current {} [{}]",
   697	                                    name,
   698	                                    through.reference,
   699	                                    old_name,
   700	                                    old_id,
   701	                                    through.parent,
   702	                                    current.unwrap_or("<missing>")
   703	                                ),
   704	                            );
   705	                            return Ok(());
   706	                        }
   707	                    }
   708	                    if let Ok(Value::Id(parent_id)) = row.field(&through.reference) {
   709	                        if through.live.is_some() || crate::rows::Rows::restored_live_authority(tx,name,id)? {
   710	                            let bytes:Option<i64>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT octet_length(data) FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![parent_t,parent_id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   711	                            meter.heap((bytes.unwrap_or(0) as u64).saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(4096))?;
   712	                        }
   713	                        if let Some(parent) = tx.get(&through.parent, parent_id)? {
   714	                            let parent =
   715	                                crate::schema::row_image(&parent, &next.tables[&through.parent]);
   716	                            // @ref LLP 3000.014.000#71-dormant-restoration-identities-are-a-relation — live policy restamps current inputs after preserving parent lifetime.
   717	                            if through.live.is_none() && !crate::rows::Rows::restored_live_authority(tx,name,id)? && through.fields.iter().any(|(capture, field)| {
   718	                                row.field(capture)
   719	                                    .ok()
   720	                                    .is_some_and(|value| Some(value) != parent.field(field).ok())
   721	                            }) {
   722	                                bad.add(id,&format!("{}.{} parent {parent_id}: captured audience/identity changed",name,through.reference));
   723	                            }
   724	                        }
   725	                    }
   726	                    Ok(())
   727	                })?;
   728	                if bad.count > 0 {
   729	                    return Err(refuse(format!(
   730	                        "table {name}: {}; restore cannot rebind historical audience/deadline captures; restore the original parent reference and audience under the current release, then `snapback4 rollback <dir> --to N`, or explicitly `snapback4 prune <dir> --allow-destructive`",
   731	                        bad.describe()
   732	                    )));
   733	                }
   734	            }
   735	        }
   736	    }
   737	    Ok(())
   738	}
   739	
   740	pub(super) fn delete(tx: &Tx<'_>, t: i64, id: &str, name: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   741	    super::backfills::delete(tx,t,id)?;
   742	    if !enabled(tx)? {
   743	        return Ok(());
   744	    }
   745	    tx.conn().execute(
   746	        "UPDATE _dormant_refs SET invalid=1 WHERE parent_t=?1 AND parent_id=?2 AND invalid=0",
   747	        params![t, id],
   748	    )?;
   749	    let narrowed=super::narrowed::present(tx.conn())?;
   750	    let home=if narrowed {" OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?4 AND id=?2)"} else {""};
   751	    tx.conn().execute(&format!("UPDATE _assets SET released_at=COALESCE(released_at,?3) WHERE home_table=?1 AND home_id=?2 AND (home_column IN (SELECT column_name FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?4 AND id=?2){home})"),params![name,id,crate::now_ms(),t])?;
   752	    tx.conn().execute(
   753	        "DELETE FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",
   754	        params![t, id],
   755	    )?;
   756	    tx.conn().execute(
   757	        "DELETE FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",
   758	        params![t, id],
   759	    )?;
   760	    if narrowed {tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id])?;}
   761	    tx.conn().execute(
   762	        "DELETE FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",
   763	        params![t, id],
   764	    )?;
   765	    Ok(())
   766	}

   768	pub(super) fn holds_home(tx: &Tx<'_>, table: &str, column: &str) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
   769	    if !enabled(tx)? {
   770	        return Ok(false);
   771	    }

   773	    Ok(tx.conn().query_row(&format!("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_tables WHERE table_name=?1) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_columns WHERE table_name=?1 AND column_name=?2){narrowed}"),params![table,column],|r|r.get(0))?)
   774	}

   776	/// Fetch only destination cells on live transitions, after paying their full
   777	/// serialized/decoded coexistence in the owning activation page/row account.
   778	pub(super) fn restored_values(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,table:&Table,meter:&mut crate::meter::Meter)->Result<Vec<(String,String)>,RuntimeError> {
   779	    if !super::live_activation::active(tx)? {
   780	        return Ok(tx.conn().prepare_cached("SELECT column_name,data FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2")?.query_map(params![t,id],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?);
   781	    }
   782	    let mut values=Vec::new();
   783	    for column in table.columns.keys() {
   784	        let bytes:Option<i64>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT octet_length(data) FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",params![t,id,column],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   785	        if let Some(bytes)=bytes {
   786	            meter.heap((bytes as u64).saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(1024+column.len() as u64*8))?;
   787	            let data:String=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",params![t,id,column],|r|r.get(0))?;
   788	            values.push((column.clone(),data));
   789	        }
   790	    }
   791	    Ok(values)
   792	}

//! The generation barrier checks predecessor audiences before destructive projection.
// @ref LLP 3000.014#7-activation — pinned time, child scan, capture before validation, atomic publication.
use crate::{
    error::RuntimeError,
    rows::{Bounds, Dir, Rows},
    schema::{RuleExpr, Schema, Table, through::PARENT_DEADLINE},
    value::Value,
};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

const EXPIRY_TABLE_CURSOR: &str = "$expiry-table";

/// Rotate a sweep's table order after the last table that consumed a bounded
/// visit. Per-table row cursors alone can starve every later table when an
/// earlier table contains a permanently restricted due row.
pub(crate) fn resume_expiry_tables(
    mut tables: Vec<String>,
    cursors: &BTreeMap<String, Vec<Value>>,
) -> Vec<String> {
    let after = cursors
        .get(EXPIRY_TABLE_CURSOR)
        .and_then(|cursor| cursor.first())
        .and_then(|value| value.as_str().ok());
    if let Some(index) = after.and_then(|after| tables.iter().position(|table| table == after)) {
        let next = (index + 1) % tables.len().max(1);
        tables.rotate_left(next);
    }
    tables
}

pub(crate) fn mark_expiry_table(cursors: &mut BTreeMap<String, Vec<Value>>, table: &str) {
    cursors.insert(EXPIRY_TABLE_CURSOR.into(), vec![Value::String(table.into())]);
}

/// Only inherited batches need staging. Preserve the streaming path for
/// existing schemas, including large fixture imports.
pub(crate) fn ancestor_order<'a, I>(
    schema: &Schema,
    rows: I,
) -> Box<dyn Iterator<Item = (String, Value)> + 'a>
where
    I: IntoIterator<Item = (String, Value)>,
    I::IntoIter: 'a,
{
    if schema.tables.values().all(|t| t.through.is_none()) {
        return Box::new(rows.into_iter());
    }
    let order = schema.through_order();
    let mut rows: Vec<_> = rows.into_iter().collect();
    rows.sort_by_key(|(name, _)| order.iter().position(|n| n == name).unwrap_or(usize::MAX));
    Box::new(rows.into_iter())
}

fn refusal(name: &str, count: usize, reason: &str, samples: &[String]) -> RuntimeError {
    RuntimeError::Structural {
        code: "E_READ_THROUGH_ADOPT",
        message: format!(
            "{name}: {count} child rows cannot adopt read through: {reason}; sample ids/parent values [{}]; rewrite: reconcile the named audience fields and live parents in a bridge release, then `snapback4 deploy <dir> --restore-dormant` (guide rules)",
            samples.join(", ")
        ),
    }
}

fn sample(samples: &mut Vec<String>, row: &Value, reference: &str, parent: &str) {
    fn bounded(text: &str) -> &str {
        let mut end = text.len().min(256);
        while !text.is_char_boundary(end) {
            end -= 1;
        }
        &text[..end]
    }
    if samples.len() < 5 {
        let id = row
            .field("id")
            .ok()
            .and_then(|v| v.as_str().ok())
            .unwrap_or("<missing>");
        let value = row
            .field(reference)
            .ok()
            .and_then(|v| v.as_str().ok())
            .unwrap_or("<missing>");
        samples.push(format!(
            "{}: {reference} -> {parent}[{}]",
            bounded(id),
            bounded(value)
        ));
    }
}

fn live_work(tx:&dyn Rows,name:&str,old:Option<&Table>,next:&Schema)->Result<bool,RuntimeError> {
    Ok(old.and_then(|t|t.through.as_ref()).is_some_and(|t|t.live.is_some())
        || next.table(name).and_then(|t|t.through.as_ref()).is_some_and(|t|t.live.is_some())
        || tx.has_restored_live_authority(name)?)
}

// Both definitions may coexist in a raw source image until projection.
fn parent_bytes(a:Option<&Table>,b:Option<&Table>)->u64 {
    a.into_iter().chain(b).map(crate::rows::disclosed::decode_reservation).fold(0_u64,u64::saturating_add).saturating_mul(4)
}

fn rows(
    tx: &dyn Rows, name: &str, table: &Table, parent_scratch: u64, bounded: bool, destination: Option<&Table>,
    mut visit: impl FnMut(Value) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    if !bounded {
        let mut bounds=Bounds::default();
        loop {
            let page=tx.scan(name,"byId",&[],&bounds,Dir::Asc,256)?;
            if page.is_empty() {return Ok(());}
            for (row,key) in page {bounds.after=crate::key::decode(&key);visit(row)?;}
        }
    }
    let decode=crate::rows::disclosed::decode_reservation(table);
    let keys=crate::rows::adoption::index_scratch(name,table).saturating_add(destination.map_or(0,|t|crate::rows::adoption::index_scratch(name,t)));
    let entries=crate::rows::adoption::index_capacity(table).saturating_add(destination.map_or(0,crate::rows::adoption::index_capacity)).saturating_mul(2);
    let scratch=decode.saturating_mul(4).saturating_add(destination.map_or(0,crate::rows::disclosed::decode_reservation).saturating_mul(4)).saturating_add(keys.saturating_mul(2))
        .saturating_add(parent_scratch).saturating_add(8192);
    tx.walk_adoption(name,decode,scratch,[2,table.columns.len() as u64+16,entries],&mut visit)
}

pub(crate) fn correspondence(
    child: &RuleExpr,
    parent: &RuleExpr,
) -> Option<std::collections::BTreeMap<String, String>> {
    if child.is_uniformly_readable() && parent.is_uniformly_readable() {
        return Some(Default::default());
    }
    fn walk(
        a: &serde_json::Value,
        b: &serde_json::Value,
        map: &mut std::collections::BTreeMap<String, String>,
    ) -> bool {
        use serde_json::Value::*;
        match (a, b) {
            (Object(a), Object(b)) if a.len() == b.len() => {
                if let (Some(String(a)), Some(String(b))) = (a.get("Field"), b.get("Field")) {
                    if map.get(a).is_some_and(|old| old != b)
                        || map.iter().any(|(old, v)| v == b && old != a)
                    {
                        return false;
                    }
                    map.insert(a.clone(), b.clone());
                    true
                } else {
                    a.iter()
                        .all(|(k, a)| b.get(k).is_some_and(|b| walk(a, b, map)))
                }
            }
            (Array(a), Array(b)) if a.len() == b.len() => {
                a.iter().zip(b).all(|(a, b)| walk(a, b, map))
            }
            _ => a == b,
        }
    }
    let mut map = Default::default();
    walk(
        &serde_json::to_value(child).ok()?,
        &serde_json::to_value(parent).ok()?,
        &mut map,
    )
    .then_some(map)
}

/// The retirement phase shared by activation and its read-only occupancy
/// preview. Selection and the predecessor deadline predicate live here once;
/// checking a candidate must not count a row this phase would remove.
pub(crate) struct Retirement<'a> {
    affected: Vec<(String, &'a Table)>,
    now: i64,
}

impl<'a> Retirement<'a> {
    pub(crate) fn new(next: &Schema, previous: &'a Schema, now: i64) -> Self {
        let affected = next
            .through_order()
            .into_iter()
            .filter_map(|name| {
                let next = &next.tables[&name];
                let old = previous.table(&name)?;
                Self::changes_lifetime(next,old).then_some((name, old))
            })
            .collect();
        Self { affected, now }
    }

    fn changes_lifetime(next:&Table,old:&Table) -> bool {
        next.through != old.through || next.through.is_some() && (next.expire != old.expire || next.expire.as_ref().is_some_and(|field|old.defaults.get(field)!=next.defaults.get(field)))
    }

    pub(crate) fn retires_image(next:&Table,old:&Table,row:&Value,now:i64) -> bool {
        (next.through!=old.through || next.expire!=old.expire || old.expire.as_ref().is_some_and(|field|old.defaults.get(field)!=next.defaults.get(field))) && old.expired(row,now)
    }

    pub(crate) fn affects(&self, name: &str) -> bool {
        self.affected.iter().any(|(table, _)| table == name)
    }

    pub(crate) fn retires(&self, name: &str, row: &Value) -> bool {
        self.affected
            .iter()
            .find(|(table, _)| table == name)
            .is_some_and(|(_, old)| old.expired(row, self.now))
    }
}

   345	
   346	// Reset even when a debug/unwind request panics mid-drain. Engine poison
   347	// recovery and durable work cursors retain the normal retry semantics.
   348	struct Advancing<'a>(&'a Shared);
   349	impl Drop for Advancing<'_> {
   350	    fn drop(&mut self) {
   351	        let mut state = lock(&self.0.maintenance);
   352	        state.advancing = false;
   353	        state.next = BulkKind::Delivery;
   354	    }
   355	}
   356	
   357	fn completed_drain(work: Work, stopping: bool) -> Result<Work, snapback4_core::Refusal> {
   358	    if stopping {
   359	        return Err(snapback4_core::Refusal::new("E_CLOCK_INTERRUPTED", "clock", "clock advanced, but host shutdown interrupted the drain; inspect queued work instead of repeating the advance").guide("clock"));
   360	    }
   361	    Ok(work)
   362	}
   363	
   364	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#4-staging-clock — staging grants clock control, never fixture or persona authority.
   365	pub(super) fn staging(shared: &Shared) -> bool {
   366	    lock(&shared.engine).store().meta("deployment_environment").ok().flatten().as_deref() == Some("staging")
   367	}
   368	fn clock_service(request: &Request, shared: &Shared) -> Result<Option<String>, snapback4_core::Refusal> {
   369	    if shared.personas { return Ok(None); }
   370	    if !staging(shared) { return Err(crate::dev_clock::release()); }
   371	    match super::session_identity::principal_of(request,shared) {
   372	        Ok((principal,kind,snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Full)) if kind == "service" && principal.starts_with("service:") => Ok(Some(principal)),
   373	        _ => Err(snapback4_core::Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "staging clock control requires a full service token").guide("clock")),
   374	    }
   375	}
   376	pub(super) fn advance(request: Request, shared: &Shared, body: &str) {
   377	    let service = match clock_service(&request, shared) {
   378	        Ok(service) => service,
   379	        Err(why) => return respond(request,403,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)})),
   380	    };
   381	    let payload = match serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(body) {
   382	        Ok(payload) => payload,
   383	        Err(_) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("clock advance takes {\"duration\":\"+2m\"}"))})),
   384	    };
   385	    let each_boundary = match payload.get("eachBoundary") {
   386	        None => false,
   387	        Some(serde_json::Value::Bool(value)) => *value,
   388	        Some(_) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("eachBoundary must be a boolean"))})),
   389	    };
   390	    let duration = payload.get("duration").and_then(|value| value.as_str());
   391	    let milliseconds = match duration.ok_or_else(|| crate::dev_clock::input("clock advance takes {\"duration\":\"+2m\"}"))
   392	        .and_then(crate::dev_clock::duration) {
   393	        Ok(milliseconds) => milliseconds,
   394	        Err(error) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
   395	    };
   396	    // Serialize clock callers only. Background slices check advancing after
   397	    // engine admission and skip during this synchronous drain.
   398	    let advance = lock(&shared.maintenance).advance.clone();
   399	    let _advance = lock(&advance);
   400	    let _advancing = Advancing(shared);
   401	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12 — simulated time makes retries due without stealing a live worker's claim or viewer session.
   402	    let advanced = (|| {
   403	        // Match claims' engine-then-clock lock order: no claim can land
   404	        // between shifting its lease and publishing the advanced clock.
   405	        let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
   406	        let before = crate::dev_clock_store::saved_boundaries(&engine);
   407	        if let Some(principal) = &service {
   408	            if engine.store().meta("deployment_environment").map_err(|e|e.refusal())?.as_deref() != Some("staging") { return Err(crate::dev_clock::release()); }
   409	            super::session_identity::mutation_authority(&request,shared,&engine,principal)?;
   410	        } else { shared.dev_authority(&engine)?; }
   411	        let mut clock = lock(&shared.clock);
   412	        let mut next = clock.clone();
   413	        let now = next.advance(milliseconds)?.max(shared.time_audience_clock.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Acquire));
   414	        let boundaries = crate::dev_clock_store::crossed_boundaries(&engine, &before, now, shared.hold_schedules, each_boundary)?;
   415	        lock(&shared.maintenance).advancing = true;
   416	        engine.advance_worker_leases_at(milliseconds, clock.now()).map_err(|e| e.refusal())?;
   417	        *clock = next;
   418	        let offset = clock.offset();
   419	        let skew = now - snapback4_core::now_ms();
   420	        let isolated = if crate::dev_clock_store::has_data(&engine) {
   421	            format!("; use {} for isolated time tests; clock advance --port N targets its printed port (guide jobs)", crate::dev_clock::isolated_command())
   422	        } else { String::new() };
   423	        let message = format!("cumulative offset {offset:+}ms (requested process offset; now includes the authoritative floor); process offset lasts until restart{isolated}");
   424	        Ok((now, offset, skew, message, before, boundaries))
   425	    })();
   426	    let (now, offset, skew, message, before, boundaries) = match advanced {
   427	        Ok(result) => result,
   428	        Err(error) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
   429	    };
   430	    // @ref LLP 3000#contended-read-serving — a developer is awaiting this
   431	    // request; synchronous clock-advance work intentionally keeps request QoS.
   432	    let drained = (|| {
   433	        let mut crossed = Vec::new();
   434	        if each_boundary {
   435	            for (index, at) in boundaries {
   436	                crossed.extend(engine_work(shared, |engine, _| engine.fire_schedule_boundary_at(shared.now(), index, at))?);
   437	            }
   438	        }
   439	        let mut work = work_at(shared, true, 0, Some(now))?;
   440	        work.schedules += crossed.len();
   441	        work.fired.extend(crossed);
   442	        Ok::<_, RuntimeError>(work)
   443	    })();
   444	    match drained.map_err(|error| error.refusal()).and_then(|work| completed_drain(work, crate::lifecycle::stopping())) {
   445	        Ok(work) => {
   446	            let schedules = crate::dev_clock_store::schedule_report(&lock(&shared.engine), &before, &work.fired, now, shared.hold_schedules, each_boundary, shared.dev_identity.as_ref().map(|i| i.address.port()));
   447	            respond(request, 200, json!({"now":now, "advancedMs":milliseconds, "offsetMs":offset, "skewMs":skew, "eachBoundary":each_boundary, "message":message, "work":work, "schedules":schedules}))
   448	        }
   449	        // Time remains advanced if maintenance fails; callers must not
   450	        // retry the advance blindly. The normal tick retries pending work.
   451	        Err(error) => respond(request, 500, json!({"now":now, "advancedMs":milliseconds, "offsetMs":offset, "skewMs":skew, "eachBoundary":each_boundary, "message":message, "denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
   452	    }
   453	}

   455	/// `POST /dev/jobs/<job>` with `{"args":{..}}`: queue one run of a job as the
   456	/// system on the dev clock and drive chunks until it is done or parked. This is
   457	/// what `snapback4 run <job>` forwards to while dev owns the store.
   458	pub(super) fn run_job(request: Request, shared: &Shared, name: &str, body: &str) {
   459	    use snapback4_core::{JobState, error::Refusal, ir::ProgramKind, wire};
   460	    let service_route=request.url().starts_with("/jobs/");
   461	    let service=if service_route {
   462	        match super::session_identity::principal_of(&request,shared) {
   463	            Ok((principal,kind,snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Full)) if kind=="service" && principal.starts_with("service:") => Some(principal),
   464	            _ => return respond(request,403,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH","auth","triggering a deployed system job requires a full service token").guide("jobs"))})),
   465	        }
   466	    } else {
   467	        if !shared.personas {return respond(request,403,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH","auth","/dev/jobs is a development door; use run <job> --release <dir> with a service token").guide("jobs"))}));}
   468	        None
   469	    };
   470	    let refuse = |request, status, refusal: Refusal| respond(request, status, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&refusal.guide("jobs"))}));
   471	    let payload: serde_json::Value = match serde_json::from_str(if body.trim().is_empty() { "{}" } else { body }) {
   472	        Ok(payload) => payload,
   473	        Err(error) => return refuse(request, 400, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("the request body is not JSON: {error}; send {{\"args\":{{..}}}}"))),
   474	    };
   475	    if service_route && !shared.personas {crate::release_requests::Scope::admit(&shared.release_requests,||*lock(&shared.generation));}
   476	    let program = lock(&shared.bound).programs.iter().find(|p| p.program.name == name).map(|p| p.program.clone());
   477	    let program = match program {
   478	        Some(program) if program.kind == ProgramKind::Job => program,
   479	        Some(other) => return refuse(request, 404, Refusal::new("E_OP", "op", format!("{name} is a {}, not a job; queries are POST /q/<op>, mutations POST /m/<op>", other.kind.word()))),
   480	        None => return respond(request, 404, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&super::dev_doors::stale(shared, Refusal::new("E_OP", "op", format!("unknown job {name}")).guide("jobs")))})),
   481	    };
   482	    if let Some(raw)=payload.get("args") && let Some(why)=super::dev_doors::persona_argument(shared,&program.args,raw) {return refuse(request,400,why);}
   483	    let mut args = std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
   484	    if let Some(fields) = payload.get("args").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object) {
   485	        for (field, value) in fields {
   486	            let Some(kind) = program.args.get(field) else { return refuse(request, 400, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("{name} takes no argument {field}"))); };
   487	            match wire::from_json(kind, value) {
   488	                Ok(typed) => { args.insert(field.clone(), typed); }
   489	                Err(why) => return refuse(request, 400, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("argument {field}: {why}"))),
   490	            }
   491	        }
   492	    }
   493	    let mut admitted=lock(&shared.engine);
   494	    crate::release_requests::stamp(admitted.generation());
   495	    if !service_route && let Err(why) = shared.dev_authority(&admitted) { drop(admitted); return refuse(request,403,why); }
   496	    if let Some(service)=&service {
   497	        if let Err(why)=super::session_identity::mutation_authority(&request,shared,&admitted,service) {drop(admitted);return refuse(request,403,why);}
   498	    }
   499	    let queued=admitted.enqueue_job(name,args,shared.now());
   500	    drop(admitted);
   501	    crate::release_requests::executed();
   502	    let id = match queued {
   503	        Ok(id) => id,
   504	        Err(error) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error.refusal())})),
   505	    };
   506	    // FIFO: earlier queued runs finish first, as with `run` on a stopped store;
   507	    // the background tick may run some chunks too, through the same engine.
   508	    let run = loop {
   509	        let current = {let engine=lock(&shared.engine);crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());engine.job(&id)};
   510	        match current {
   511	            Ok(Some(run)) if matches!(run.state, JobState::Done | JobState::Parked) || crate::lifecycle::stopping() => break run,
   512	            Ok(Some(_)) => {}
   513	            Ok(None) => return respond(request, 500, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&RuntimeError::Store(format!("job {id} vanished")).refusal())})),
   514	            Err(error) => return respond(request, 500, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error.refusal())})),
   515	        }
   516	        let (chunk, logs) = engine_work(shared, |engine, budget| (engine.run_job_chunk_for(shared.now(), budget), engine.take_native_logs()));
   517	        for line in logs { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: native {line}"); }
   518	        match chunk {
   519	            Ok(Some(chunk)) if chunk.state == JobState::Parked && shared.personas => shared.refusals.job(&chunk),
   520	            Ok(_) => {}
   521	            Err(error) => return respond(request, 500, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error.refusal())})),
   522	        }
   523	    };
   524	    snapback4_core::diagnostic!("snapback4: job {} {} on request: {} chunk{}, {} row{} written", run.name, if run.state == JobState::Done { "done" } else { "parked" },
   525	        run.chunks, if run.chunks == 1 { "" } else { "s" }, run.rows_written, if run.rows_written == 1 { "" } else { "s" });
     1	//! A process-local clock for development and explicitly classified staging stores.
     2	use snapback4_core::error::Refusal;
     3	use std::time::{Duration, Instant};
     4	
     5	const MAX_TIME: i64 = snapback4_core::value::I53_MAX;
     6	
     7	pub fn input(message: impl Into<String>) -> Refusal { Refusal::new("E_CLOCK_INPUT", "clock", message).guide("clock") }
     8	pub fn release() -> Refusal { Refusal::new("E_CLOCK_DEV_ONLY", "clock", "clock controls require dev or a deploy --staging store; production always uses wall time").guide("clock") }
     9	
    10	/// Isolated time tests stay inside an assigned range, or request a free port.
    11	pub(crate) fn isolated_command() -> String {
    12	    isolated_range(std::env::var("PORT_LO").ok().as_deref(), std::env::var("PORT_HI").ok().as_deref())
    13	}
    14	fn isolated_range(lo: Option<&str>, hi: Option<&str>) -> String {
    15	    match (lo.and_then(|n| n.parse::<u16>().ok()), hi.and_then(|n| n.parse::<u16>().ok())) {
    16	        (Some(lo), Some(hi)) if lo > 0 && hi >= lo => format!("dev --memory --port {lo}-{hi} within the current range"),
    17	        _ => "dev --memory --port 0".into(),
    18	    }
    19	}
    20	
    21	pub fn duration(text: &str) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
    22	    if text.starts_with('-') { return Err(input("clock advance cannot move backwards; use a positive duration such as +2m")); }
    23	    let text = text.strip_prefix('+').unwrap_or(text);
    24	    let split = text.bytes().position(|b| !b.is_ascii_digit()).unwrap_or(text.len());
    25	    let (number, unit) = text.split_at(split);
    26	    let multiplier = match unit { "ms" => 1, "s" => 1_000, "m" => 60_000, "h" => 3_600_000, "d" => 86_400_000, "w" => 604_800_000, _ => 0 };
    27	    number.parse::<i64>().ok().and_then(|n| n.checked_mul(multiplier)).filter(|n| *n > 0 && *n <= MAX_TIME)
    28	        .ok_or_else(|| input("expected a positive whole duration with ms, s, m, h, d or w, such as +25h"))
    29	}
    30	
    31	pub fn offset(text: &str) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
    32	    if let Some(rest) = text.strip_prefix('-') { duration(rest).map(|n| -n) } else { duration(text) }
    33	}
    34	
    35	fn time(value: Option<i64>) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
    36	    value.filter(|n| (0..=MAX_TIME).contains(n)).ok_or_else(|| input("time must be Unix milliseconds in 0..9007199254740991; choose a smaller time or duration"))
    37	}
    38	
    39	pub fn run_now(text: Option<&String>) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
    40	    let now = snapback4_core::now_ms();
    41	    match text {
    42	        None => Ok(now),
    43	        Some(text) if text.starts_with('+') => time(now.checked_add(duration(text)?)),
    44	        Some(text) => time(text.parse().ok()),
    45	    }
    46	}
    47	
    48	#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
    49	pub struct Clock {
    50	    dev: bool,
    51	    base: i64,
    52	    offset: i64,
    53	    started: Instant,
    54	    epoch: String,
    55	    revision: u64,
    56	    #[cfg(test)]
    57	    frozen: bool,
    58	}
    59	
    60	impl Clock {
    61	    pub fn new(dev: bool, offset: i64) -> Result<Self, Refusal> {
    62	        if !dev && offset != 0 { return Err(release()); }
    63	        Ok(Self { dev, offset, base: time(snapback4_core::now_ms().checked_add(offset))?, started: Instant::now(),
    64	            epoch: snapback4_core::ids::mint(snapback4_core::now_ms(), &mut snapback4_core::ids::Mint::default()).map_err(|e| e.refusal())?, revision: 0, #[cfg(test)] frozen: false })
    65	    }
    66	    #[cfg(test)]
    67	    pub(crate) fn freeze(&mut self, now: i64) { self.base = now; self.frozen = true; }
    68	    pub fn now(&self) -> i64 {
    69	        #[cfg(test)] if self.frozen { return self.base; }
    70	        if !self.dev { return snapback4_core::now_ms(); }
    71	        self.base.saturating_add(self.started.elapsed().as_millis().min(MAX_TIME as u128) as i64).min(MAX_TIME)
    72	    }
    73	    /// Identifies samples taken before/after a restart or deliberate clock jump.
    74	    pub fn offset(&self) -> i64 { self.offset }
    75	    pub fn revision(&self) -> String { format!("{}:{}", self.epoch, self.revision) }
    76	    pub fn presence_now(&self) -> i64 {
    77	        // Production grace is elapsed real time, unaffected by wall-clock corrections.
    78	        if self.dev { self.now() } else { self.started.elapsed().as_millis().min(MAX_TIME as u128) as i64 }
    79	    }
    80	    /// Back to wall time: the offset `--clock-offset` and every advance added
    81	    /// is dropped (a development reset's `{"clock":"reset"}`).
    82	    pub fn reset(&mut self) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
    83	        if !self.dev { return Err(release()); }
    84	        self.revision += 1;
    85	        self.offset = 0;
    86	        self.base = snapback4_core::now_ms();
    87	        self.started = Instant::now();
    88	        #[cfg(test)] { self.frozen = false; }
    89	        Ok(self.base)
    90	    }
    91	    pub fn advance(&mut self, milliseconds: i64) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
    92	        if !self.dev { return Err(release()); }
    93	        if milliseconds <= 0 { return Err(input("clock advance cannot move backwards; use a positive duration such as +2m")); }
    94	        let next = time(self.now().checked_add(milliseconds))?;
    95	        let offset = self.offset.checked_add(milliseconds).ok_or_else(|| input("cumulative clock offset overflow"))?;
    96	        self.revision += 1;
    97	        self.offset = offset;
    98	        self.base = next;
    99	        self.started = Instant::now();
   100	        Ok(next)
   101	    }
   102	}
   103	
   104	/// The control CLI only talks to a loopback dev host. It does not open its store.
   105	pub fn command(options: &crate::verbs::Options) -> i32 {
   106	    let result = (|| -> Result<String, String> {
   107	        if options.positionals.len() != 2 || options.positionals[0] != "advance" {
   108	            return Err(input(format!("usage: {}", crate::cli_flags::synopsis("clock"))).to_string());
   109	        }
   110	        duration(&options.positionals[1]).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   111	        let release_path = options.flags.get("release").map(|dir| std::path::PathBuf::from(dir).join("store.sqlite"));
   112	        if release_path.is_some() && options.flags.contains_key("port") { return Err("clock --release does not accept --port".into()); }
   113	        let (address, instance, token) = if let Some(path) = &release_path {
   114	            let store = snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(path).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
   115	            if store.meta("deployment_environment").map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.as_deref() != Some("staging") { return Err(release().to_string()); }
   116	            let address = crate::store_owner::inspector_address(path).ok_or("staging clock requires a running serve")?;
   117	            let session = crate::serve::token::mint_live(path, "clock", 1).map_err(|(_,error)|error)?;
   118	            (address,None,Some(session.token))
   119	        } else { let (address,instance)=crate::verbs::dev_address(options)?; (address,instance,None) };
   120	        let body = serde_json::json!({"duration":options.positionals[1],"eachBoundary":options.flags.contains_key("each-boundary")}).to_string();
   121	        // The common HTTP/1.0 exchange is EOF-delimited and bounds replies at
   122	        // 64 MiB, enough for the preflight's complete explicit window report.
   123	        let response = if let Some(token) = &token {
   124	            crate::verbs::run_forward::exchange_authorized(address,"POST","/dev/clock/advance",None,Some(token),&body,Duration::from_secs(60))
   125	        } else { crate::verbs::run_forward::exchange_dev(address, "/dev/clock/advance", instance.as_deref(), &body, Duration::from_secs(60)) };
   126	        let (status, json) = response.map_err(|error| {
   127	            let evidence = crate::verbs::run_forward::exchange(address, "GET", "/dev/status", None, "", Duration::from_secs(5)).ok()
   128	                .map(|(_, status)| format!("now={}, skewMs={}", status["now"], status["skewMs"]))
   129	                .unwrap_or_else(|| "clock status unavailable".into());
   130	            format!("clock advance reply failed at {address}: {error}; time may already have advanced ({evidence}); inspect /dev/status and queued work before retrying; for an isolated dev pass --port N")
   131	        })?;
   132	        if let Some(denied) = json.get("denied") {
   133	            let clock = json["message"].as_str().map(|message| format!("; time remains advanced: {message}")).unwrap_or_default();
   134	            return Err(format!("{}: {}{clock}\n  guide: clock", denied["code"].as_str().unwrap_or("E_CLOCK_INPUT"), denied["message"].as_str().unwrap_or("clock advance failed")));
   135	        }
   136	        if status != 200 { return Err(format!("dev refused clock advance: {json}")); }
   137	        Ok(json.to_string())
   138	    })();
   139	    match result { Ok(body) => { println!("{body}"); 0 }, Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); 1 } }
   140	}
   141	
   142	#[cfg(test)]
   143	mod isolated_tests {
   144	    #[test]
   145	    fn time_test_guidance_uses_only_valid_assigned_ranges_or_a_free_port() {
   146	        assert_eq!(super::isolated_range(Some("24000"), Some("24009")), "dev --memory --port 24000-24009 within the current range");
   147	        for (lo,hi) in [(None,None),(Some("0"),Some("3")),(Some("4"),Some("3")),(Some("bad"),Some("3"))] {
   148	            assert_eq!(super::isolated_range(lo,hi), "dev --memory --port 0");
   149	        }
   150	    }
   151	}
     1	//! Host startup; request scheduling remains in serve.rs.
     2	use super::*;
     3	pub fn serve(
     4	    project: Project,
     5	    port: u16,
     6	    mut mode: Mode,
     7	    bind: &str,
     8	    inherited: Option<std::net::TcpListener>,
     9	    capacity: crate::host_limits::Capacity,
    10	) -> Result<(), String> {
    11	    crate::host_limits::initialize();
    12	    let capacity = capacity.fit()?;
    13	    crate::host_limits::install(capacity);
    14	    let mut source_stamp = project.sources_stamp();
    15	    let bound = project.compile().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    16	    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
    17	        let verbose = !matches!(mode, Mode::Dev { verbose: false, .. });
    18	        let (reports, hidden) = crate::verbs::problem_reports(&bound, true, verbose);
    19	        let shown = reports.len();
    20	        for report in reports {
    21	            snapback4_core::diagnostic!("{report}");
    22	        }
    23	        if hidden > 0 {
    24	            snapback4_core::diagnostic!(
    25	                "snapback4 dev: {hidden} redundant cascade diagnostic(s) hidden; use --verbose to show them"
    26	            );
    27	        }
    28	        return Err(format!(
    29	            "{shown} problem{} in snapback/",
    30	            if shown == 1 { "" } else { "s" }
    31	        ));
    32	    }
    33	    let dev = matches!(mode, Mode::Dev { .. });
    34	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.2 — replay suppresses new effects and holds schedule admission; ordinary holds keep their separate meanings.
    35	    let replay = matches!(mode, Mode::Dev { replay: true, .. });
    36	    let hold_effects = matches!(
    37	        mode,
    38	        Mode::Dev {
    39	            hold_effects: true,
    40	            ..
    41	        }
    42	    );
    43	    let hold_schedules = replay
    44	        || matches!(
    45	            mode,
    46	            Mode::Dev {
    47	                hold_schedules: true,
    48	                ..
    49	            }
    50	        );
    51	    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#4-staging-clock — only immutable store classification admits a release test clock.
    52	    let staging = if let Mode::Release { owner, .. } = &mode {
    53	        owner.open_store(Duration::from_secs(5)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.meta("deployment_environment").map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.as_deref() == Some("staging")
    54	    } else { false };
    55	    let clock = crate::dev_clock::Clock::new(
    56	        dev || staging,
    57	        match mode {
    58	            Mode::Dev { clock_offset, .. } | Mode::Release { clock_offset, .. } => clock_offset,
    59	        },
    60	    )
    61	    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    62	    let generated = if dev {
    63	        Some(project.write_generated(&bound).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?)
    64	    } else {
    65	        None
    66	    };
    67	    if dev {
    68	        project.check_server_at(&mut source_stamp)?;
    69	    }
    70	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.2 — only the owning host may replace replay/schedule policy; deployment shares the same lock.
    71	    // Release startup already owns this lock before selecting its source.
    72	    let mut host_lock = if matches!(mode, Mode::Dev { fresh: false, .. }) {
    73	        Some(crate::store_owner::host(&project.store_path())?)
    74	    } else {
    75	        None
    76	    };
    77	    // A busy port is a failed start, not permission to change the stopped host's policy.
    78	    let server =
    79	        crate::witness_socket::server(bind, port, inherited, capacity.connections(), || {
    80	            crate::listen_refusal::in_use(bind, port, if dev { "dev" } else { "serve" })
    81	        })?;
    82	    let address = server
    83	        .server_addr()
    84	        .to_ip()
    85	        .ok_or("HTTP listener has no IP address")?;
    86	    if let Some(file) = &mut host_lock {
    87	        crate::store_owner::announce(file, address)?;
    88	    }
    89	    if let Mode::Release { owner, .. } = &mut mode {
    90	        // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — publish through the acquired handle, including a release database symlink.
    91	        crate::store_owner::announce(
    92	            Arc::get_mut(owner).ok_or("release owner already shared")?,
    93	            address,
    94	        )?;
    95	    }
    96	    let host_owner = match &mode {
    97	        Mode::Release { owner, .. } => Some(owner.clone()),
    98	        _ => host_lock.take().map(Arc::new),
    99	    };
   100	    if let Some(owner) = &host_owner { owner.report_reclaim(matches!(mode, Mode::Dev { verbose: true, .. })); }
   101	    // `--fresh` has private, disposable storage and the same WAL readers as dev.
   102	    let fresh = if matches!(mode, Mode::Dev { fresh: true, .. }) {
   103	        Some(query_readers::FreshDirectory::new()?)
   104	    } else {
   105	        None
   106	    };
   107	    let mut engine = match &mode {
   108	        Mode::Dev { fresh: true, .. } => Engine::new_at(
   109	            fresh.as_ref().unwrap().store().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
   110	            bound.schema.clone(),
   111	            bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()),
   112	            clock.now(),
   113	        ),
   114	        Mode::Dev { .. } => adoption::open(host_owner.as_deref().unwrap(), &bound, &clock),
   115	        Mode::Release { expected, .. } => host_owner
    85	        (Ok(None), Ok(None)) => (None, None),
    86	        (Ok(Some(server)), Ok(Some(proof))) => match protocol::nonce(server) {
    87	            Some(server) => (Some(server), Some(proof.to_owned())), None => return denied(request),
    88	        },
    89	        _ => return denied(request),
    90	    };
    91	    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
    92	    if request.as_reader().take((BODY_LIMIT + 1) as u64).read_to_end(&mut bytes).is_err() || bytes.len() > BODY_LIMIT {
    93	        return denied(request);
    94	    }
    95	    let mint: Mint = match serde_json::from_slice::<Mint>(&bytes) {
    96	        Ok(mint) if mint.store == authority.store => mint,
    97	        _ => return denied(request),
    98	    };
    99	    let binding = mint.binding();
   100	    let Some(server) = server else {
   101	        let Some(server) = authority.challenge(client, binding.clone(), std::time::Instant::now()) else { return denied(request); };
   102	        return respond_with_cors(request, 200, json!({"server_nonce": protocol::hex(&server),
   103	            "server_proof": protocol::server_proof(&authority.secret, &client, &server, &binding)}), false);
   104	    };
   105	    if !authority.consume(&client, &server, &binding, proof.as_deref().unwrap(), std::time::Instant::now()) {
   106	        return denied(request);
   107	    }
   108	    let result = {
   109	        // Use the same writer and identity routine as offline minting. Sample
   110	        // wall time as the offline CLI does, independent of dev's test clock.
   111	        let engine = producing_engine(shared);
   112	        shared.owner.as_ref().expect("authorized owner").path().and_then(|_| {
   113	            let now = if engine.store().meta("deployment_environment")?.as_deref() == Some("staging") { shared.now() } else { snapback4_core::now_ms() };
   114	            identity::service_session(engine.store(), &mint.service, now, mint.days)
   115	        })
   116	    };
   117	    let reply = match result {
   118	        Ok(session) => json!({"session": session}),
   119	        Err(error) => {
   120	            let why = error.refusal().site(ENDPOINT).rewrite(if matches!(error, snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Auth(_)) {
   121	                "use --service with 1–64 letters, digits, _ or -"
   122	            } else { "inspect the store error, repair the store access, then rerun snapback4 token" }).guide("services");
   123	            json!({"denied": why})
   124	        }
   125	    };
   126	    let Some(sealed) = protocol::seal(&authority.secret, &client, &server, &binding, &serde_json::to_vec(&reply).unwrap()) else {
   127	        return denied(request);
   128	    };
   129	    respond_with_cors(request, 200, json!({"sealed": sealed}), false);
   130	}
   131	
   132	fn exchange(address: std::net::SocketAddr, headers: &str, body: &str) -> io::Result<Json> {
   133	    let mut stream = TcpStream::connect_timeout(&address, Duration::from_secs(1))?;
   134	    stream.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(10)))?;

//! Row authority and dormant cell provenance are separate facts.
// @ref LLP 3000.014.000#implementation-proof-clarifications — validate the immediate manual policy before using older live evidence.
use super::*;
use crate::schema::through::{PARENT_DEADLINE, DEADLINE};

pub(super) fn mode(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str)->Result<Option<u8>,RuntimeError> {
    if !live_activation::active(tx)? {return Ok(None);}
    Ok(tx.conn().query_row("SELECT CASE kind WHEN 'source-manual' THEN 0 WHEN 'source-captured' THEN 1 ELSE 2 END FROM temp._live_restored WHERE kind IN ('source-manual','source-captured','source-live') AND t=?1 AND col='' AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?)
}
pub(super) fn record(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,old:&Table)->Result<(),RuntimeError> {
    if !live_activation::active(tx)? {return Ok(());}
    let kind=match &old.through {None=>"source-manual",Some(e) if e.live.is_some()=>"source-live",Some(_)=>"source-captured"};
    tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._live_restored VALUES(?1,?2,'',?3)",params![kind,t,id])?;
    if let Some(edge)=old.through.as_ref().filter(|e|e.live.is_none()) {
        for col in edge.fields.keys().map(String::as_str).chain([PARENT_DEADLINE,DEADLINE]) {
            tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._live_restored VALUES('source-capture',?1,?2,?3)",params![t,col,id])?;
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}
fn live_cell(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,col:&str)->Result<bool,RuntimeError> {
    Ok(tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3 AND live_source=1)",params![t,id,col],|r|r.get(0))?)
}
/// Captured-only restoration preserves its old audience. A manual row with
/// live-origin inputs must prove its *current* audience before recapture.
pub(super) fn validate(tx:&Tx<'_>,next:&Schema,sources:&BTreeMap<String,Table>,name:&str,old:&Table,row:&Value,now:i64,narrowed:bool)->Result<(),RuntimeError> {
    let Some(table)=next.table(name) else {return Ok(())};
    let t=tx.num(name)?;let id=row.field("id")?.as_str()?;
    if needs_proof(tx,t,id,old,table,narrowed)? {
        crate::engine::through_activation::forward_authority(tx,next,sources,name,old,row,now,true)?;
        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._live_restored VALUES('live-authority',?1,'',?2)",params![t,id])?;
    }
    Ok(())
}
/// Never let unused live cells replace a captured row's own audience/deadline.
/// Missing live-origin inputs remain absent until the capture barrier fills
/// just those new inputs from the current parent.
pub(super) fn overlay(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,old:Option<&Table>,table:&Table,row:&mut Value,values:Vec<(String,String)>)->Result<(),RuntimeError> {
    let captured=old.and_then(|t|t.through.as_ref()).filter(|e|e.live.is_none());
    for (col,data) in values {
        if !table.columns.contains_key(&col) {continue;}
        let protected=captured.is_some_and(|e|e.fields.contains_key(&col) || matches!(col.as_str(),PARENT_DEADLINE|DEADLINE));
        if protected {continue;}
        let stale=captured.is_some() && live_activation::active(tx)? && live_cell(tx,t,id,&col)?;
        if let Value::Row(fields)|Value::Map(fields)=row {
            if stale {fields.remove(&col);} else {fields.insert(col,serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?);}
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}
pub(super) fn live_input(table:&Table,column:&str)->bool {
    table.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|e|e.live.is_some() && (e.fields.contains_key(column) || matches!(column,PARENT_DEADLINE|DEADLINE)))
}
/// Current policy inputs are refreshable; the immutable parent lifetime and
/// authored references remain bound even when old policy targets disappear.
pub(super) fn obsolete_ref(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,column:&str,table:&Table,old:Option<&Table>)->Result<bool,RuntimeError> {
    let Some(edge)=&table.through else {return Ok(false)};
    if column==PARENT_DEADLINE || !(edge.fields.contains_key(column) || column==DEADLINE) {return Ok(false);}
    let protected=if mode(tx,t,id)?.is_some() {
        tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM temp._live_restored WHERE kind='source-capture' AND t=?1 AND id=?2 AND col=?3)",params![t,id,column],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))?
    } else {old.and_then(|t|t.through.as_ref()).is_some_and(|e|e.live.is_none() && (e.fields.contains_key(column) || column==DEADLINE))};
    if protected {return Ok(false);}
    Ok(edge.live.is_some() || live_activation::active(tx)? && live_cell(tx,t,id,column)?)
}

/// Reserve declared parent scratch only for callbacks that can fetch it.
/// Captured-only restoration keeps its existing actual-image admission.
pub(super) fn forward_fetch(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,old:Option<&Table>,table:&Table)->Result<bool,RuntimeError> {
    match old {Some(old)=>needs_proof(tx,t,id,old,table,false),None=>Ok(false)}
}
/// One predicate for actual parent fetches and their preflight reservation.
fn needs_proof(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,old:&Table,table:&Table,narrowed:bool)->Result<bool,RuntimeError> {
    if !live_activation::active(tx)? || old.through.is_some() {return Ok(false);}
    let Some(edge)=&table.through else {return Ok(false)};
    if edge.live.is_some() {return Ok(true);}
    for col in edge.fields.keys().map(String::as_str).chain([PARENT_DEADLINE,DEADLINE]) {
        if live_cell(tx,t,id,col)? {return Ok(true);}
    }
    if !narrowed {return Ok(false);}
    let history:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3 AND live_source=0)",params![t,id,PARENT_DEADLINE],|r|r.get(0))?;
    Ok(!history)
}
pub(super) fn live_row(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,old:&Table,table:&Table)->Result<bool,RuntimeError> {
    if !live_activation::active(tx)? {return Ok(false);}
    if old.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|e|e.live.is_some()) || table.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|e|e.live.is_some()) {return Ok(true);}
    for col in table.columns.keys() {if live_cell(tx,t,id,col)? {return Ok(true);}}
    needs_proof(tx,t,id,old,table,true)

    /// Whether a row is past its table's `expire` column at this run's
    /// clock. Expired is absent: to every reader, on every host, before
    /// any rule (the sweep makes it physical). The system sees no more
    /// than anyone: an expired row contributes to nothing.
    pub(super) fn expired(&self, table: &Table, row: &Value) -> bool {
        self.context.now.is_some_and(|now| table.expired(row, now))
    }

    /// Whether a stored row is outside its published half-open read lifetime.
    /// Physical write resolution and cleanup use `expired` instead. A due row
    /// remains absent while its private opening entry is still pending: the
    /// bounded publisher has not emitted this row's partition transition yet.
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.003#4-advancing-time-without-an-application-job — a due ledger entry is the publication barrier.
    fn inactive_with_pending(&mut self, table_name: &str, row: &Value, refuse_pending: bool) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        let Some(now) = self.context.now else { return Ok(false); };
        let table = &self.schema.tables[table_name];
        if !table.live(row, now) { return Ok(true); }
        if table.not_before.is_none() { return Ok(false); }
        let id = row.field("id")?.as_str()?;
        if !self.tx.time_audience_pending(table_name, id)? { return Ok(false); }
        if refuse_pending {
            self.query_unavailable = true;
            self.pending_opening = Some((table_name.to_owned(), id.to_owned()));
            return Err(RuntimeError::TimeAudiencePending);
        }
        Ok(true)
    }

    pub(super) fn inactive(&mut self, table_name: &str, row: &Value) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        self.inactive_with_pending(table_name, row, true)
    }

    pub(super) fn inactive_write_target(&mut self, table_name: &str, row: &Value) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        self.inactive_with_pending(table_name, row, false)
    }

//! Column admission, maintained fields and authority/identity/delivery dependencies.
// @ref LLP 3000.007.001#2 — the binder and raw-schema admission share this walk.
use super::*;

pub const GROUP: &str = "$parent";

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct ColumnOwner {
    pub table: String,
    pub column: String,
}

impl ColumnOwner {
    pub fn label(&self) -> String { format!("{}.{}", self.table, self.column) }
    // Bounded labels also keep a first contribution independent of parent state.
    pub fn target(&self) -> String { format!("$column:{}", digest(&(&self.table, &self.column))) }
}

impl Schema {
    pub(crate) fn private_owner(&self, name: &str) -> Option<&ColumnOwner> {
        // Privacy also holds before admission: a duplicate ordinary declaration
        // must never shadow a private one in a raw schema.
        self.maintains.iter().filter(|m| m.target == name).find_map(|m| m.private.as_ref())
    }

    pub fn visible(&self, name: &str) -> bool { self.private_owner(name).is_none() && !self.is_recount_work(name) }

    pub fn visible_tables(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = (&String, &Table)> {
        self.tables.iter().filter(|(name, _)| self.visible(name))
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.007.001#3.2 — identity remains on Table, which image-retention compares.
    pub fn public_projection(&self) -> Self {
        let mut schema = self.clone();
        #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
        schema.backfills.clear();
        // Inspection provenance is catalog side metadata, never device input.
        #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
        { schema.authored_indexes = Default::default(); }
        schema.prepare_receipt_dependencies();
        schema.receipt_dependencies.retain(|name, _| self.visible(name));
        schema.source_reservations.retain(|name, _| self.visible(name));
        schema.tables.retain(|name, _| self.visible(name));
        schema.maintains.retain(|m| self.visible(&m.target));
        schema
    }

    pub fn refuse_private(&self, name: &str) -> Result<(), crate::error::RuntimeError> {
        if self.is_recount_work(name) { return Err(crate::error::RuntimeError::Structural {code:"E_VIEW_KEY",message:"recount state is engine-owned; read the row-keyed view instead (guide maintains)".into()}); }
        if let Some(owner) = self.private_owner(name) {
            return Err(crate::error::RuntimeError::Structural { code: "E_MAINTAINED_COLUMN_PRIVATE",
                message: format!("{} is maintained by the engine; read the parent row or write the source rows (guide maintains)", owner.label()) });
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    /// A projected device schema deliberately has metadata without private maintains.
    /// Authoritative engines must have exactly one derivation for every owned field.
    pub fn validate_column_owners(&self) -> Result<(), crate::error::RuntimeError> {
        for (name,table) in self.visible_tables() {
            for column in table.maintained.keys() {
                if self.maintains.iter().filter(|m|m.private.as_ref().is_some_and(|o|&o.table == name && &o.column == column)).count() != 1 {
                    return Err(crate::error::RuntimeError::Schema(format!("{name}.{column} needs exactly one engine derivation; declare the column aggregate (guide maintains)")));
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

impl Maintain {
    pub fn group_fields(&self) -> Vec<String> {
        if self.private.is_some() { vec![GROUP.into()] } else { self.by.clone() }
    }

    pub fn derivation_identity(&self) -> String { digest(&(&self.kind, &self.source, &self.filter, &self.by)) }
    pub fn label(&self) -> String { self.private.as_ref().map_or_else(|| self.target.clone(), ColumnOwner::label) }
}

//! Server activation builder; all fallible work precedes commit.
use super::*;
impl Engine {
    pub(super) fn activate(
        store: &mut Store,
        schema: &Schema,
        programs: &BTreeMap<String, Program>,
        release: Option<&release::ReleaseActivation>,
        now: i64,
        allow_feed_adoption: bool,
        expected_release: Option<u64>,
        expected_backend: Option<&crate::store::opening::Backend>,
    ) -> Result<(u64, crate::store::opening::Metadata), RuntimeError> {
        schema.validate_column_owners()?;
        for program in programs.values() {
            crate::schema::ephemeral::admit_program(schema, program)?;
            admission::check_state_predicates(schema, program)?;
        }
        for program in programs.values() {
            for table in crate::ir::read_sites(program)
                .into_iter()
                .map(|s| s.table)
                .chain(crate::ir::write_tables(program).into_iter().map(|(t, _)| t))
            {
                schema.refuse_private(&table)?;
            }
        }
        for schedule in &schema.schedules {
            if !schedule.valid()
            {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(format!(
                    "schedule {} has an invalid period or offset",
                    schedule.name
                )));
            }
            let Some(program) = programs
                .get(&schedule.target)
                .filter(|p| matches!(p.kind, ProgramKind::Job | ProgramKind::Action))
            else {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(format!(
                    "schedule {} names missing job or action {}; restore or remove the schedule",
                    schedule.name, schedule.target
                )));
            };
            if program.kind == ProgramKind::Action {
                let seat = schedule
                    .service
                    .as_deref()
                    .filter(|seat| crate::identity::is_service(seat))
                    .ok_or_else(|| {
                        RuntimeError::Schema(
                            "a scheduled action requires its declared service seat".into(),
                        )
                    })?;
                actions::authorize(program, seat)?;
            } else if schedule.service.is_some() {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(
                    "a scheduled job cannot name a service seat".into(),
                ));

                !matches!(name.as_str(), "periodStart" | "periodEnd")

                conn.execute("INSERT INTO _meta(key,value) VALUES('activation_restore',?1) ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value=excluded.value",[if options.restore_dormant {"true"}else{"false"}])?;
            }
            Ok(())
        }, |tx, changed, report| {
            if let Some(options)=release {tx.set_meta("release",&options.release.to_string())?;}

            let previous: Option<String> = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT schema FROM _generations WHERE generation = (SELECT CAST(value AS INTEGER) FROM _meta WHERE key = 'generation')", [], |row| row.get(0)).optional()?;
            let previous: Option<Schema> = previous.map(|text| serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::decode(e, RuntimeError::Store))).transpose()?;
            let needs_maintained_id_marker = (schema.maintains.iter().any(|maintain| maintain.row_key.is_none())
                || previous.as_ref().is_some_and(|old| old.maintains.iter().any(|maintain| maintain.row_key.is_none())))
                && tx.meta(crate::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_META)?.as_deref() != Some(crate::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_VERSION);
            if !changed && !repair_live_sums && !needs_maintained_id_marker { return Ok(()); }
            let repair_maintained_ids = needs_maintained_id_marker
                && previous.as_ref().is_some_and(|old| old.maintains.iter().any(|maintain| maintain.row_key.is_none()));
            if changed {
                let epoch = tx.next_seq()?;
                for table in partition::changed_group_audiences(previous.as_ref(), schema) {
                    tx.set_meta(&partition::audience_epoch_key(&table), &epoch.to_string())?;
                    tx.set_meta(&partition::audience_epoch_time_key(&table), &now.to_string())?;
                }
            }
            let population_changed=tx.meta("activation_population")?.as_deref()==Some("true");
            let rebuild: Vec<_> = schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m| {
                (changed && (population_changed || previous.as_ref().is_none_or(|old| crate::successor::maintain_rebuild_needed(old, schema, m))))
                    || (repair_maintained_ids && m.row_key.is_none())
                    || (repair_live_sums && matches!(m.kind, crate::schema::MaintainKind::Sum { .. }) && schema.table(&m.source).is_some_and(|source| source.is_expiring()))
            }).collect();
            tx.set_meta("live_sum_candidates", "1")?;
            if needs_maintained_id_marker {
                // This write is inside the activation transaction: a failed
                // rebuild leaves both old rows and the old/missing marker. A
                // first aggregate has no legacy target identity to rewrite,
                // but records the current address function for later opens.

            }
            let attribution = recorded::paths_changed(schema, previous.as_ref());
            let columns = column_activation::changed_parents(schema, if population_changed {None} else {previous.as_ref()});
